================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\ppld\evaluate_ppld_saved_model.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from PIL import Image
from torchvision import models, transforms
from sklearn.metrics import (
    accuracy_score,
    classification_report,
    confusion_matrix,
)


# ============================================================
# PATHS
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

MODEL_PATH = (
    PROJECT_ROOT
    / "models"
    / "best_ppld_efficientnet_b0.pth"
)

TEST_DIR = (
    PROJECT_ROOT
    / "PPLD_processed"
    / "test"
)

RESULTS_PATH = (
    PROJECT_ROOT
    / "models"
    / "ppld_saved_model_evaluation.txt"
)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device(
    "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
)

print("=" * 70)
print("TurSathi AI - Saved PPLD Model Full Evaluation")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Device : {DEVICE}")

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print(f"GPU    : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")

print("=" * 70)


# ============================================================
# CHECK PATHS
# ============================================================

if not MODEL_PATH.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Model not found: {MODEL_PATH}"
    )

if not TEST_DIR.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Test directory not found: {TEST_DIR}"
    )


# ============================================================
# LOAD CHECKPOINT
# ============================================================

checkpoint = torch.load(
    MODEL_PATH,
    map_location=DEVICE,
    weights_only=False,
)

classes = checkpoint["classes"]
num_classes = len(classes)

print("\nModel:")
print(f"  Name             : {checkpoint['model_name']}")
print(f"  Classes          : {classes}")
print(f"  Best Val Accuracy: "
      f"{checkpoint['best_val_accuracy'] * 100:.2f}%")
print(f"  Best Epoch       : {checkpoint['best_epoch']}")


# ============================================================
# CREATE MODEL
# ============================================================

model = models.efficientnet_b0(
    weights=None
)

in_features = model.classifier[1].in_features

model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
    in_features,
    num_classes,
)

model.load_state_dict(
    checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
)

model = model.to(DEVICE)
model.eval()


# ============================================================
# IMAGE TRANSFORM
# ============================================================

transform = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((224, 224)),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


# ============================================================
# COLLECT TEST IMAGES
# ============================================================

image_extensions = {
    ".jpg",
    ".jpeg",
    ".png",
}

test_images = []

for class_name in classes:

    class_dir = TEST_DIR / class_name

    if not class_dir.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Class directory not found: {class_dir}"
        )

    for image_path in sorted(class_dir.iterdir()):

        if image_path.suffix.lower() in image_extensions:

            test_images.append(
                (image_path, class_name)
            )


print("\nTest images found:")
print(f"  Total: {len(test_images)}")

for class_name in classes:

    count = sum(
        1
        for _, actual in test_images
        if actual == class_name
    )

    print(
        f"  {class_name}: {count}"
    )


# ============================================================
# FULL INFERENCE
# ============================================================

all_actual = []
all_predicted = []

correct = 0

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("RUNNING INFERENCE")
print("=" * 70)


with torch.no_grad():

    for index, (image_path, actual_class) in enumerate(
        test_images,
        start=1,
    ):

        image = Image.open(
            image_path
        ).convert("RGB")

        tensor = transform(
            image
        ).unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE)

        output = model(tensor)

        probabilities = torch.softmax(
            output,
            dim=1,
        )[0]

        predicted_index = probabilities.argmax().item()

        predicted_class = classes[
            predicted_index
        ]

        all_actual.append(
            actual_class
        )

        all_predicted.append(
            predicted_class
        )

        if predicted_class == actual_class:
            correct += 1


        # Progress
        if index % 25 == 0 or index == len(test_images):

            print(
                f"Processed: {index}/{len(test_images)}"
            )


# ============================================================
# ACCURACY
# ============================================================

accuracy = accuracy_score(
    all_actual,
    all_predicted,
)

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL SAVED MODEL EVALUATION")
print("=" * 70)

print(
    f"\nCorrect predictions : {correct}/{len(test_images)}"
)

print(
    f"Incorrect predictions: "
    f"{len(test_images) - correct}/{len(test_images)}"
)

print(
    f"Test Accuracy       : "
    f"{accuracy * 100:.2f}%"
)


# ============================================================
# CLASSIFICATION REPORT
# ============================================================

report = classification_report(
    all_actual,
    all_predicted,
    labels=classes,
    target_names=classes,
    digits=4,
    zero_division=0,
)

print("\nClassification Report:")
print(report)


# ============================================================
# CONFUSION MATRIX
# ============================================================

matrix = confusion_matrix(
    all_actual,
    all_predicted,
    labels=classes,
)

print("Confusion Matrix:")

print(
    "                "
    + " ".join(
        f"{name[:10]:>12}"
        for name in classes
    )
)

for class_name, row in zip(
    classes,
    matrix,
):

    print(
        f"{class_name[:15]:<15}"
        + " ".join(
            f"{value:>12}"
            for value in row
        )
    )


# ============================================================
# SAVE EVALUATION REPORT
# ============================================================

with open(
    RESULTS_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    file.write(
        "TurSathi AI - Saved PPLD Model Evaluation\n"
    )

    file.write(
        "=" * 60 + "\n\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Model: {checkpoint['model_name']}\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Best Validation Accuracy: "
        f"{checkpoint['best_val_accuracy'] * 100:.2f}%\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Best Epoch: "
        f"{checkpoint['best_epoch']}\n\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Total Test Images: "
        f"{len(test_images)}\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Correct Predictions: "
        f"{correct}\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Incorrect Predictions: "
        f"{len(test_images) - correct}\n"
    )

    file.write(
        f"Test Accuracy: "
        f"{accuracy * 100:.2f}%\n\n"
    )

    file.write(
        "Classification Report:\n"
    )

    file.write(
        report
    )

    file.write(
        "\nConfusion Matrix:\n"
    )

    file.write(
        "Classes: "
        + ", ".join(classes)
        + "\n"
    )

    for row in matrix:

        file.write(
            " ".join(
                map(str, row)
            )
            + "\n"
        )


# ============================================================
# FINAL
# ============================================================

print("\nEvaluation report saved:")
print(RESULTS_PATH)

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("EVALUATION COMPLETE")
print("=" * 70)

================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\ppld\test_ppld_model.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from PIL import Image
from torchvision import models, transforms


# ============================================================
# PATHS
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

MODEL_PATH = PROJECT_ROOT / "models" / "best_ppld_efficientnet_b0.pth"

IMAGE_PATH = (
    PROJECT_ROOT
    / "PPLD_processed"
    / "test"
    / "Healthy"
    / "Healthy_0000.jpg"
)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device(
    "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
)

print("=" * 70)
print("TurSathi AI - PPLD Saved Model Inference Test")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Device : {DEVICE}")

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print(f"GPU    : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")

print("=" * 70)


# ============================================================
# LOAD CHECKPOINT
# ============================================================

if not MODEL_PATH.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Model not found: {MODEL_PATH}"
    )

if not IMAGE_PATH.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Image not found: {IMAGE_PATH}"
    )


checkpoint = torch.load(
    MODEL_PATH,
    map_location=DEVICE,
    weights_only=False,
)

classes = checkpoint["classes"]

num_classes = len(classes)

print("\nModel information:")
print(f"Model              : {checkpoint.get('model_name', 'Unknown')}")
print(f"Classes            : {classes}")
print(f"Number of classes  : {num_classes}")
print(
    f"Best validation accuracy: "
    f"{checkpoint.get('best_val_accuracy', 0) * 100:.2f}%"
)
print(
    f"Best epoch         : "
    f"{checkpoint.get('best_epoch', 'Unknown')}"
)


# ============================================================
# CREATE MODEL
# ============================================================

model = models.efficientnet_b0(
    weights=None
)

in_features = model.classifier[1].in_features

model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
    in_features,
    num_classes,
)

model.load_state_dict(
    checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
)

model = model.to(DEVICE)

model.eval()


# ============================================================
# IMAGE TRANSFORMATION
# ============================================================

transform = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((224, 224)),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


# ============================================================
# LOAD IMAGE
# ============================================================

image = Image.open(
    IMAGE_PATH
).convert("RGB")

input_tensor = transform(
    image
).unsqueeze(0)

input_tensor = input_tensor.to(
    DEVICE
)


# ============================================================
# INFERENCE
# ============================================================

with torch.no_grad():

    output = model(
        input_tensor
    )

    probabilities = torch.softmax(
        output,
        dim=1,
    )[0]

    predicted_index = probabilities.argmax().item()

    predicted_class = classes[
        predicted_index
    ]

    confidence = probabilities[
        predicted_index
    ].item()


# ============================================================
# RESULTS
# ============================================================

actual_class = IMAGE_PATH.parent.name

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("INFERENCE RESULT")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Image     : {IMAGE_PATH.name}")
print(f"Actual    : {actual_class}")
print(f"Predicted : {predicted_class}")
print(f"Confidence: {confidence * 100:.2f}%")

print("\nAll class probabilities:")

for index, class_name in enumerate(classes):

    probability = probabilities[
        index
    ].item() * 100

    print(
        f"  {class_name:<20} "
        f"{probability:.2f}%"
    )


# ============================================================
# RESULT CHECK
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)

if predicted_class == actual_class:

    print("RESULT: CORRECT PREDICTION")

else:

    print("RESULT: INCORRECT PREDICTION")

print("=" * 70)

================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\ppld\train_ppld.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path
import copy
import json

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torchvision import datasets, models, transforms
from torchvision.models import EfficientNet_B0_Weights

from sklearn.metrics import (
    classification_report,
    confusion_matrix,
    accuracy_score,
)


# ============================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DATASET_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed"
MODEL_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "models"
MODEL_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

BEST_MODEL_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "best_ppld_efficientnet_b0.pth"
RESULTS_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "ppld_training_results.json"
CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "ppld_confusion_matrix.txt"

IMAGE_SIZE = 224
BATCH_SIZE = 16
NUM_WORKERS = 0

EPOCHS = 30
LEARNING_RATE = 1e-4
WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4

EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = 7

SEED = 42


# ============================================================
# REPRODUCIBILITY
# ============================================================

torch.manual_seed(SEED)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")

print("=" * 70)
print("TurSathi AI - PPLD EfficientNet-B0 Training")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Project root : {PROJECT_ROOT}")
print(f"Dataset      : {DATASET_DIR}")
print(f"Device       : {DEVICE}")

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print(f"GPU          : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    print(f"CUDA         : {torch.version.cuda}")
    print(f"VRAM         : {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 1024**3:.2f} GB")

print("=" * 70)


# ============================================================
# DATA TRANSFORMS
# ============================================================

train_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.RandomHorizontalFlip(p=0.5),
    transforms.RandomVerticalFlip(p=0.2),
    transforms.RandomRotation(15),

    transforms.ColorJitter(
        brightness=0.15,
        contrast=0.15,
        saturation=0.15,
        hue=0.03,
    ),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


eval_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


# ============================================================
# DATASETS
# ============================================================

train_dir = DATASET_DIR / "train"
val_dir = DATASET_DIR / "val"
test_dir = DATASET_DIR / "test"

if not train_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Training directory not found: {train_dir}")

if not val_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Validation directory not found: {val_dir}")

if not test_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Test directory not found: {test_dir}")


train_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    train_dir,
    transform=train_transforms,
)

val_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    val_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)

test_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    test_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)


# ============================================================
# CLASS CHECK
# ============================================================

class_names = train_dataset.classes
num_classes = len(class_names)

print("\nClasses:")
for index, class_name in enumerate(class_names):
    print(f"  {index}: {class_name}")

print(f"\nNumber of classes: {num_classes}")

if class_names != val_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and validation class order does not match.")

if class_names != test_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and test class order does not match.")


# ============================================================
# DATALOADERS
# ============================================================

train_loader = DataLoader(
    train_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=True,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

val_loader = DataLoader(
    val_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

test_loader = DataLoader(
    test_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)


print("\nDataset sizes:")
print(f"  Train: {len(train_dataset)}")
print(f"  Val  : {len(val_dataset)}")
print(f"  Test : {len(test_dataset)}")


# ============================================================
# MODEL
# ============================================================

print("\nLoading EfficientNet-B0 pretrained weights...")

weights = EfficientNet_B0_Weights.DEFAULT

model = models.efficientnet_b0(weights=weights)

# Replace final classifier
in_features = model.classifier[1].in_features

model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
    in_features,
    num_classes,
)

model = model.to(DEVICE)


# ============================================================
# LOSS / OPTIMIZER
# ============================================================

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

optimizer = optim.AdamW(
    model.parameters(),
    lr=LEARNING_RATE,
    weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY,
)

scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(
    optimizer,
    mode="max",
    factor=0.5,
    patience=2,
)


# ============================================================
# TRAINING FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def train_one_epoch(model, loader, criterion, optimizer):
    model.train()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    for images, labels in loader:

        images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
        labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)

        outputs = model(images)

        loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

        loss.backward()

        optimizer.step()

        running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

        predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

        correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
        total += labels.size(0)

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return epoch_loss, epoch_accuracy


# ============================================================
# VALIDATION FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def evaluate(model, loader, criterion):
    model.eval()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    all_labels = []
    all_predictions = []

    with torch.no_grad():

        for images, labels in loader:

            images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
            labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

            outputs = model(images)

            loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

            running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

            predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

            correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
            total += labels.size(0)

            all_labels.extend(labels.cpu().numpy())
            all_predictions.extend(predictions.cpu().numpy())

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return (
        epoch_loss,
        epoch_accuracy,
        all_labels,
        all_predictions,
    )


# ============================================================
# TRAINING LOOP
# ============================================================

best_val_accuracy = 0.0
best_epoch = 0
patience_counter = 0

best_model_state = None

history = {
    "train_loss": [],
    "train_accuracy": [],
    "val_loss": [],
    "val_accuracy": [],
}


print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("STARTING TRAINING")
print("=" * 70)


for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):

    train_loss, train_accuracy = train_one_epoch(
        model,
        train_loader,
        criterion,
        optimizer,
    )

    (
        val_loss,
        val_accuracy,
        _,
        _,
    ) = evaluate(
        model,
        val_loader,
        criterion,
    )

    scheduler.step(val_accuracy)

    history["train_loss"].append(train_loss)
    history["train_accuracy"].append(train_accuracy)

    history["val_loss"].append(val_loss)
    history["val_accuracy"].append(val_accuracy)

    current_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]

    print(
        f"Epoch [{epoch:02d}/{EPOCHS}] | "
        f"Train Loss: {train_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Train Acc: {train_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"Val Loss: {val_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Val Acc: {val_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"LR: {current_lr:.2e}"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SAVE BEST MODEL
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if val_accuracy > best_val_accuracy:

        best_val_accuracy = val_accuracy
        best_epoch = epoch
        patience_counter = 0

        best_model_state = copy.deepcopy(model.state_dict())

        checkpoint = {
            "model_name": "efficientnet_b0",
            "classes": class_names,
            "num_classes": num_classes,
            "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
            "best_val_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
            "model_state_dict": best_model_state,
        }

        torch.save(
            checkpoint,
            BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        )

        print(
            f"  -> Best model saved "
            f"(Val Acc: {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%)"
        )

    else:

        patience_counter += 1

        print(
            f"  -> No improvement "
            f"({patience_counter}/{EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE})"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # EARLY STOPPING
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if patience_counter >= EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE:

        print("\nEarly stopping triggered.")

        break


# ============================================================
# LOAD BEST MODEL
# ============================================================

if best_model_state is not None:

    model.load_state_dict(best_model_state)

else:

    checkpoint = torch.load(
        BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        map_location=DEVICE,
        weights_only=False,
    )

    model.load_state_dict(
        checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL TEST EVALUATION
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL TEST EVALUATION")
print("=" * 70)

(
    test_loss,
    test_accuracy,
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
) = evaluate(
    model,
    test_loader,
    criterion,
)


print(f"\nTest Loss     : {test_loss:.4f}")
print(f"Test Accuracy : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")


# ============================================================
# CLASSIFICATION REPORT
# ============================================================

report = classification_report(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
    target_names=class_names,
    digits=4,
    zero_division=0,
)

print("\nClassification Report:")
print(report)


# ============================================================
# CONFUSION MATRIX
# ============================================================

cm = confusion_matrix(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
)

print("Confusion Matrix:")
print(cm)

with open(
    CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    file.write("Classes:\n")

    for index, name in enumerate(class_names):
        file.write(f"{index}: {name}\n")

    file.write("\nConfusion Matrix:\n")

    for row in cm:
        file.write(" ".join(map(str, row)) + "\n")


# ============================================================
# SAVE TRAINING RESULTS
# ============================================================

results = {
    "model": "EfficientNet-B0",
    "dataset": "PPLD_processed",
    "classes": class_names,
    "num_classes": num_classes,
    "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
    "batch_size": BATCH_SIZE,
    "epochs_requested": EPOCHS,
    "best_epoch": best_epoch,
    "best_validation_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
    "test_loss": test_loss,
    "test_accuracy": test_accuracy,
    "train_samples": len(train_dataset),
    "validation_samples": len(val_dataset),
    "test_samples": len(test_dataset),
    "history": history,
}

with open(
    RESULTS_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    json.dump(
        results,
        file,
        indent=4,
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("TRAINING COMPLETE")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Best validation accuracy : {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")
print(f"Best epoch              : {best_epoch}")
print(f"Test accuracy           : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")

print(f"\nBest model:")
print(BEST_MODEL_PATH)

print(f"\nTraining results:")
print(RESULTS_PATH)

print(f"\nConfusion matrix:")
print(CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH)

print("=" * 70)

================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\ppld\train_ppld_backup_before_clean_training.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path
import copy
import json

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torchvision import datasets, models, transforms
from torchvision.models import EfficientNet_B0_Weights

from sklearn.metrics import (
    classification_report,
    confusion_matrix,
    accuracy_score,
)


# ============================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DATASET_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed"
MODEL_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "models"
MODEL_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

BEST_MODEL_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "best_ppld_efficientnet_b0.pth"
RESULTS_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "ppld_training_results.json"
CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "ppld_confusion_matrix.txt"

IMAGE_SIZE = 224
BATCH_SIZE = 16
NUM_WORKERS = 0

EPOCHS = 30
LEARNING_RATE = 1e-4
WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4

EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = 7

SEED = 42


# ============================================================
# REPRODUCIBILITY
# ============================================================

torch.manual_seed(SEED)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")

print("=" * 70)
print("TurSathi AI - PPLD EfficientNet-B0 Training")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Project root : {PROJECT_ROOT}")
print(f"Dataset      : {DATASET_DIR}")
print(f"Device       : {DEVICE}")

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print(f"GPU          : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    print(f"CUDA         : {torch.version.cuda}")
    print(f"VRAM         : {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 1024**3:.2f} GB")

print("=" * 70)


# ============================================================
# DATA TRANSFORMS
# ============================================================

train_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.RandomHorizontalFlip(p=0.5),
    transforms.RandomVerticalFlip(p=0.2),
    transforms.RandomRotation(15),

    transforms.ColorJitter(
        brightness=0.15,
        contrast=0.15,
        saturation=0.15,
        hue=0.03,
    ),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


eval_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


# ============================================================
# DATASETS
# ============================================================

train_dir = DATASET_DIR / "train"
val_dir = DATASET_DIR / "val"
test_dir = DATASET_DIR / "test"

if not train_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Training directory not found: {train_dir}")

if not val_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Validation directory not found: {val_dir}")

if not test_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Test directory not found: {test_dir}")


train_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    train_dir,
    transform=train_transforms,
)

val_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    val_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)

test_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    test_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)


# ============================================================
# CLASS CHECK
# ============================================================

class_names = train_dataset.classes
num_classes = len(class_names)

print("\nClasses:")
for index, class_name in enumerate(class_names):
    print(f"  {index}: {class_name}")

print(f"\nNumber of classes: {num_classes}")

if class_names != val_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and validation class order does not match.")

if class_names != test_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and test class order does not match.")


# ============================================================
# DATALOADERS
# ============================================================

train_loader = DataLoader(
    train_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=True,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

val_loader = DataLoader(
    val_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

test_loader = DataLoader(
    test_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)


print("\nDataset sizes:")
print(f"  Train: {len(train_dataset)}")
print(f"  Val  : {len(val_dataset)}")
print(f"  Test : {len(test_dataset)}")


# ============================================================
# MODEL
# ============================================================

print("\nLoading EfficientNet-B0 pretrained weights...")

weights = EfficientNet_B0_Weights.DEFAULT

model = models.efficientnet_b0(weights=weights)

# Replace final classifier
in_features = model.classifier[1].in_features

model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
    in_features,
    num_classes,
)

model = model.to(DEVICE)


# ============================================================
# LOSS / OPTIMIZER
# ============================================================

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

optimizer = optim.AdamW(
    model.parameters(),
    lr=LEARNING_RATE,
    weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY,
)

scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(
    optimizer,
    mode="max",
    factor=0.5,
    patience=2,
)


# ============================================================
# TRAINING FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def train_one_epoch(model, loader, criterion, optimizer):
    model.train()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    for images, labels in loader:

        images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
        labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)

        outputs = model(images)

        loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

        loss.backward()

        optimizer.step()

        running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

        predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

        correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
        total += labels.size(0)

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return epoch_loss, epoch_accuracy


# ============================================================
# VALIDATION FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def evaluate(model, loader, criterion):
    model.eval()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    all_labels = []
    all_predictions = []

    with torch.no_grad():

        for images, labels in loader:

            images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
            labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

            outputs = model(images)

            loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

            running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

            predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

            correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
            total += labels.size(0)

            all_labels.extend(labels.cpu().numpy())
            all_predictions.extend(predictions.cpu().numpy())

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return (
        epoch_loss,
        epoch_accuracy,
        all_labels,
        all_predictions,
    )


# ============================================================
# TRAINING LOOP
# ============================================================

best_val_accuracy = 0.0
best_epoch = 0
patience_counter = 0

best_model_state = None

history = {
    "train_loss": [],
    "train_accuracy": [],
    "val_loss": [],
    "val_accuracy": [],
}


print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("STARTING TRAINING")
print("=" * 70)


for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):

    train_loss, train_accuracy = train_one_epoch(
        model,
        train_loader,
        criterion,
        optimizer,
    )

    (
        val_loss,
        val_accuracy,
        _,
        _,
    ) = evaluate(
        model,
        val_loader,
        criterion,
    )

    scheduler.step(val_accuracy)

    history["train_loss"].append(train_loss)
    history["train_accuracy"].append(train_accuracy)

    history["val_loss"].append(val_loss)
    history["val_accuracy"].append(val_accuracy)

    current_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]

    print(
        f"Epoch [{epoch:02d}/{EPOCHS}] | "
        f"Train Loss: {train_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Train Acc: {train_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"Val Loss: {val_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Val Acc: {val_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"LR: {current_lr:.2e}"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SAVE BEST MODEL
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if val_accuracy > best_val_accuracy:

        best_val_accuracy = val_accuracy
        best_epoch = epoch
        patience_counter = 0

        best_model_state = copy.deepcopy(model.state_dict())

        checkpoint = {
            "model_name": "efficientnet_b0",
            "classes": class_names,
            "num_classes": num_classes,
            "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
            "best_val_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
            "model_state_dict": best_model_state,
        }

        torch.save(
            checkpoint,
            BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        )

        print(
            f"  -> Best model saved "
            f"(Val Acc: {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%)"
        )

    else:

        patience_counter += 1

        print(
            f"  -> No improvement "
            f"({patience_counter}/{EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE})"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # EARLY STOPPING
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if patience_counter >= EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE:

        print("\nEarly stopping triggered.")

        break


# ============================================================
# LOAD BEST MODEL
# ============================================================

if best_model_state is not None:

    model.load_state_dict(best_model_state)

else:

    checkpoint = torch.load(
        BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        map_location=DEVICE,
        weights_only=False,
    )

    model.load_state_dict(
        checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL TEST EVALUATION
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL TEST EVALUATION")
print("=" * 70)

(
    test_loss,
    test_accuracy,
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
) = evaluate(
    model,
    test_loader,
    criterion,
)


print(f"\nTest Loss     : {test_loss:.4f}")
print(f"Test Accuracy : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")


# ============================================================
# CLASSIFICATION REPORT
# ============================================================

report = classification_report(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
    target_names=class_names,
    digits=4,
    zero_division=0,
)

print("\nClassification Report:")
print(report)


# ============================================================
# CONFUSION MATRIX
# ============================================================

cm = confusion_matrix(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
)

print("Confusion Matrix:")
print(cm)

with open(
    CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    file.write("Classes:\n")

    for index, name in enumerate(class_names):
        file.write(f"{index}: {name}\n")

    file.write("\nConfusion Matrix:\n")

    for row in cm:
        file.write(" ".join(map(str, row)) + "\n")


# ============================================================
# SAVE TRAINING RESULTS
# ============================================================

results = {
    "model": "EfficientNet-B0",
    "dataset": "PPLD_processed",
    "classes": class_names,
    "num_classes": num_classes,
    "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
    "batch_size": BATCH_SIZE,
    "epochs_requested": EPOCHS,
    "best_epoch": best_epoch,
    "best_validation_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
    "test_loss": test_loss,
    "test_accuracy": test_accuracy,
    "train_samples": len(train_dataset),
    "validation_samples": len(val_dataset),
    "test_samples": len(test_dataset),
    "history": history,
}

with open(
    RESULTS_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    json.dump(
        results,
        file,
        indent=4,
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("TRAINING COMPLETE")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Best validation accuracy : {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")
print(f"Best epoch              : {best_epoch}")
print(f"Test accuracy           : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")

print(f"\nBest model:")
print(BEST_MODEL_PATH)

print(f"\nTraining results:")
print(RESULTS_PATH)

print(f"\nConfusion matrix:")
print(CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH)

print("=" * 70)

================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\prepare_ppld_dataset.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
from PIL import Image, ImageOps, ImageEnhance
import hashlib
import random
import shutil

# ============================================================
# TUR SATHI AI - PPLD DATASET PREPARATION
# ============================================================
# Original dataset is NEVER modified.
#
# Input:
#   PPLD_extracted/PPLD
#
# Output:
#   PPLD_processed/
#       train/
#       val/
#       test/
#
# Rules:
# 1. Cross-class exact duplicates are excluded.
# 2. Same-class exact duplicates are reduced to one copy.
# 3. Train / validation / test split = 70 / 15 / 15.
# 4. Augmentation is applied ONLY to training data.
# 5. Every training class is increased to at least 300 images.
# 6. Validation and test images remain original.
# ============================================================


# -----------------------------
# PATHS
# -----------------------------

SOURCE = Path(r".\PPLD_extracted\PPLD")
OUTPUT = Path(r".\PPLD_processed")

CLASSES = [
    "Healthy",
    "Leaf_Spot",
    "Leaf_webber",
    "Sterilic_mosaic",
]

IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png"}

# Reproducibility
SEED = 42
random.seed(SEED)

# Dataset split
TRAIN_RATIO = 0.70
VAL_RATIO = 0.15
TEST_RATIO = 0.15

# Minimum training images per class
MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES = 300


# ============================================================
# VALIDATION
# ============================================================

if not SOURCE.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Source dataset not found:\n{SOURCE.resolve()}"
    )

for class_name in CLASSES:
    class_path = SOURCE / class_name

    if not class_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Missing class folder:\n{class_path.resolve()}"
        )


# ============================================================
# COLLECT ORIGINAL IMAGES
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("TUR SATHI AI - PPLD DATASET PREPARATION")
print("=" * 70)

print("\nOriginal dataset:")
print(SOURCE.resolve())

print("\nOutput dataset:")
print(OUTPUT.resolve())

print("\nIMPORTANT:")
print("Original PPLD dataset will NOT be modified.")


all_images = []

for class_name in CLASSES:
    class_dir = SOURCE / class_name

    images = sorted(
        [
            p
            for p in class_dir.iterdir()
            if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS
        ]
    )

    for image_path in images:
        all_images.append((class_name, image_path))

    print(f"\n{class_name}: {len(images)} files")


print("\nTotal original image files:", len(all_images))


# ============================================================
# CALCULATE FILE HASHES
# ============================================================

print("\nChecking exact duplicate images...")

hash_to_files = defaultdict(list)

for class_name, image_path in all_images:

    with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
        image_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()

    hash_to_files[image_hash].append(
        (class_name, image_path)
    )


# ============================================================
# IDENTIFY CROSS-CLASS DUPLICATES
# ============================================================

cross_class_hashes = set()

for image_hash, files in hash_to_files.items():

    classes_found = set(
        class_name for class_name, _ in files
    )

    if len(classes_found) > 1:
        cross_class_hashes.add(image_hash)


print(
    "Cross-class duplicate groups:",
    len(cross_class_hashes)
)


# ============================================================
# CLEAN DUPLICATES
# ============================================================

# clean_images:
# only one copy of each same-class image
# cross-class conflicting duplicates are completely excluded.

clean_images = []

seen_hashes_per_class = defaultdict(set)

excluded_cross_class = 0
excluded_same_class_duplicate = 0

for class_name, image_path in all_images:

    with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
        image_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Cross-class duplicate
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    if image_hash in cross_class_hashes:
        excluded_cross_class += 1
        continue

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Same-class duplicate
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    if image_hash in seen_hashes_per_class[class_name]:
        excluded_same_class_duplicate += 1
        continue

    seen_hashes_per_class[class_name].add(image_hash)

    clean_images.append(
        (class_name, image_path)
    )


print("\nDuplicate cleaning result:")
print(
    "Excluded cross-class files:",
    excluded_cross_class
)
print(
    "Excluded same-class duplicate files:",
    excluded_same_class_duplicate
)
print(
    "Remaining clean images:",
    len(clean_images)
)


# ============================================================
# CLEAN CLASS COUNTS
# ============================================================

clean_by_class = defaultdict(list)

for class_name, image_path in clean_images:
    clean_by_class[class_name].append(image_path)


print("\nClean dataset class counts:")

for class_name in CLASSES:
    print(
        f"  {class_name:<20} "
        f"{len(clean_by_class[class_name])}"
    )


# ============================================================
# PREPARE OUTPUT DIRECTORY
# ============================================================

if OUTPUT.exists():

    existing_files = list(OUTPUT.rglob("*"))

    if existing_files:
        print("\nExisting PPLD_processed found.")
        print("Removing ONLY the previous generated output...")

        shutil.rmtree(OUTPUT)

OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


for split in ["train", "val", "test"]:

    for class_name in CLASSES:

        (OUTPUT / split / class_name).mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True
        )


# ============================================================
# SPLIT DATASET
# ============================================================

print("\nCreating Train / Validation / Test split...")

split_data = {
    "train": defaultdict(list),
    "val": defaultdict(list),
    "test": defaultdict(list),
}


for class_name in CLASSES:

    images = clean_by_class[class_name].copy()

    random.shuffle(images)

    total = len(images)

    train_count = int(total * TRAIN_RATIO)
    val_count = int(total * VAL_RATIO)

    train_images = images[:train_count]

    val_images = images[
        train_count:
        train_count + val_count
    ]

    test_images = images[
        train_count + val_count:
    ]

    split_data["train"][class_name] = train_images
    split_data["val"][class_name] = val_images
    split_data["test"][class_name] = test_images


# ============================================================
# COPY ORIGINAL TRAIN / VAL / TEST IMAGES
# ============================================================

print("\nCopying original images...")

for split in ["train", "val", "test"]:

    for class_name in CLASSES:

        images = split_data[split][class_name]

        for index, source_path in enumerate(images):

            destination = (
                OUTPUT
                / split
                / class_name
                / f"{class_name}_{index:04d}{source_path.suffix.lower()}"
            )

            shutil.copy2(
                source_path,
                destination
            )


# ============================================================
# AUGMENTATION FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def augment_image(image):
    """
    Mild agricultural leaf-image augmentation.

    Only training images are augmented.
    """

    result = image.copy()

    # Random horizontal flip
    if random.random() < 0.5:
        result = ImageOps.mirror(result)

    # Random vertical flip
    if random.random() < 0.15:
        result = ImageOps.flip(result)

    # Small rotation
    angle = random.uniform(-15, 15)

    result = result.rotate(
        angle,
        resample=Image.Resampling.BILINEAR,
        expand=False
    )

    # Brightness
    if random.random() < 0.5:

        factor = random.uniform(0.85, 1.15)

        result = ImageEnhance.Brightness(
            result
        ).enhance(factor)

    # Contrast
    if random.random() < 0.5:

        factor = random.uniform(0.85, 1.15)

        result = ImageEnhance.Contrast(
            result
        ).enhance(factor)

    # Color slightly
    if random.random() < 0.35:

        factor = random.uniform(0.90, 1.10)

        result = ImageEnhance.Color(
            result
        ).enhance(factor)

    return result


# ============================================================
# AUGMENT TRAINING DATA TO MINIMUM 300
# ============================================================

print("\nAugmenting training data...")

for class_name in CLASSES:

    train_dir = OUTPUT / "train" / class_name

    existing_train_images = [
        p
        for p in train_dir.iterdir()
        if p.is_file()
        and p.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS
    ]

    current_count = len(existing_train_images)

    print(
        f"\n{class_name}: "
        f"original train images = {current_count}"
    )

    if current_count >= MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES:

        print(
            f"Already >= {MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES}. "
            "No augmentation required."
        )

        continue

    required = MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES - current_count

    print(
        f"Generating {required} augmented images..."
    )

    source_pool = existing_train_images.copy()

    for index in range(required):

        source_path = random.choice(source_pool)

        with Image.open(source_path) as img:

            img = img.convert("RGB")

            augmented = augment_image(img)

            output_name = (
                f"{class_name}_aug_{index:04d}.jpg"
            )

            output_path = (
                train_dir / output_name
            )

            augmented.save(
                output_path,
                format="JPEG",
                quality=95
            )


# ============================================================
# FINAL COUNTS
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL DATASET COUNTS")
print("=" * 70)


for split in ["train", "val", "test"]:

    print(f"\n{split.upper()}")

    split_total = 0

    for class_name in CLASSES:

        class_dir = (
            OUTPUT / split / class_name
        )

        count = len(
            [
                p
                for p in class_dir.iterdir()
                if p.is_file()
                and p.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS
            ]
        )

        split_total += count

        print(
            f"  {class_name:<20} {count}"
        )

    print(
        f"  {'TOTAL':<20} {split_total}"
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL CHECK
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL CHECK")
print("=" * 70)

success = True

for class_name in CLASSES:

    train_dir = OUTPUT / "train" / class_name

    count = len(
        [
            p
            for p in train_dir.iterdir()
            if p.is_file()
            and p.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS
        ]
    )

    if count < MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES:

        success = False

        print(
            f"ERROR: {class_name} has only {count} "
            f"training images."
        )


if success:

    print(
        f"SUCCESS: Every training class has at least "
        f"{MIN_TRAIN_IMAGES} images."
    )

print("\nOriginal dataset was NOT modified.")

print("\nProcessed dataset:")
print(OUTPUT.resolve())

print("\nDataset preparation completed.")
print("=" * 70)

================================================================================
FILE: archive\old_training\train_pigeonpea_clean.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path
import copy
import json

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torchvision import datasets, models, transforms
from torchvision.models import EfficientNet_B0_Weights

from sklearn.metrics import (
    classification_report,
    confusion_matrix,
    accuracy_score,
)


# ============================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DATASET_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "datasets" / "pigeonpea_disease_clean"

MODEL_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "models"
MODEL_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

BEST_MODEL_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "best_pigeonpea_disease_efficientnet_b0.pth"
RESULTS_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "pigeonpea_disease_training_results.json"
CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH = MODEL_DIR / "pigeonpea_disease_confusion_matrix.txt"

IMAGE_SIZE = 224
BATCH_SIZE = 16
NUM_WORKERS = 0

EPOCHS = 30
LEARNING_RATE = 1e-4
WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4

EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = 7

SEED = 42


# ============================================================
# REPRODUCIBILITY
# ============================================================

torch.manual_seed(SEED)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")

print("=" * 70)
print("TurSathi AI - PPLD EfficientNet-B0 Training")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Project root : {PROJECT_ROOT}")
print(f"Dataset      : {DATASET_DIR}")
print(f"Device       : {DEVICE}")

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    print(f"GPU          : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    print(f"CUDA         : {torch.version.cuda}")
    print(f"VRAM         : {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / 1024**3:.2f} GB")

print("=" * 70)


# ============================================================
# DATA TRANSFORMS
# ============================================================

train_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.RandomHorizontalFlip(p=0.5),
    transforms.RandomVerticalFlip(p=0.2),
    transforms.RandomRotation(15),

    transforms.ColorJitter(
        brightness=0.15,
        contrast=0.15,
        saturation=0.15,
        hue=0.03,
    ),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


eval_transforms = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE)),

    transforms.ToTensor(),

    transforms.Normalize(
        mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225],
    ),
])


# ============================================================
# DATASETS
# ============================================================

train_dir = DATASET_DIR / "train"
val_dir = DATASET_DIR / "val"
test_dir = DATASET_DIR / "test"

if not train_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Training directory not found: {train_dir}")

if not val_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Validation directory not found: {val_dir}")

if not test_dir.exists():
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"Test directory not found: {test_dir}")


train_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    train_dir,
    transform=train_transforms,
)

val_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    val_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)

test_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(
    test_dir,
    transform=eval_transforms,
)


# ============================================================
# CLASS CHECK
# ============================================================

class_names = train_dataset.classes
num_classes = len(class_names)

print("\nClasses:")
for index, class_name in enumerate(class_names):
    print(f"  {index}: {class_name}")

print(f"\nNumber of classes: {num_classes}")

if class_names != val_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and validation class order does not match.")

if class_names != test_dataset.classes:
    raise ValueError("Train and test class order does not match.")


# ============================================================
# DATALOADERS
# ============================================================

train_loader = DataLoader(
    train_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=True,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

val_loader = DataLoader(
    val_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)

test_loader = DataLoader(
    test_dataset,
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=NUM_WORKERS,
    pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
)


print("\nDataset sizes:")
print(f"  Train: {len(train_dataset)}")
print(f"  Val  : {len(val_dataset)}")
print(f"  Test : {len(test_dataset)}")


# ============================================================
# MODEL
# ============================================================

print("\nLoading EfficientNet-B0 pretrained weights...")

weights = EfficientNet_B0_Weights.DEFAULT

model = models.efficientnet_b0(weights=weights)

# Replace final classifier
in_features = model.classifier[1].in_features

model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
    in_features,
    num_classes,
)

model = model.to(DEVICE)


# ============================================================
# LOSS / OPTIMIZER
# ============================================================

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

optimizer = optim.AdamW(
    model.parameters(),
    lr=LEARNING_RATE,
    weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY,
)

scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(
    optimizer,
    mode="max",
    factor=0.5,
    patience=2,
)


# ============================================================
# TRAINING FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def train_one_epoch(model, loader, criterion, optimizer):
    model.train()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    for images, labels in loader:

        images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
        labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)

        outputs = model(images)

        loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

        loss.backward()

        optimizer.step()

        running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

        predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

        correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
        total += labels.size(0)

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return epoch_loss, epoch_accuracy


# ============================================================
# VALIDATION FUNCTION
# ============================================================

def evaluate(model, loader, criterion):
    model.eval()

    running_loss = 0.0
    correct = 0
    total = 0

    all_labels = []
    all_predictions = []

    with torch.no_grad():

        for images, labels in loader:

            images = images.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)
            labels = labels.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

            outputs = model(images)

            loss = criterion(outputs, labels)

            running_loss += loss.item() * images.size(0)

            predictions = outputs.argmax(dim=1)

            correct += (predictions == labels).sum().item()
            total += labels.size(0)

            all_labels.extend(labels.cpu().numpy())
            all_predictions.extend(predictions.cpu().numpy())

    epoch_loss = running_loss / total
    epoch_accuracy = correct / total

    return (
        epoch_loss,
        epoch_accuracy,
        all_labels,
        all_predictions,
    )


# ============================================================
# TRAINING LOOP
# ============================================================

best_val_accuracy = 0.0
best_epoch = 0
patience_counter = 0

best_model_state = None

history = {
    "train_loss": [],
    "train_accuracy": [],
    "val_loss": [],
    "val_accuracy": [],
}


print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("STARTING TRAINING")
print("=" * 70)


for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):

    train_loss, train_accuracy = train_one_epoch(
        model,
        train_loader,
        criterion,
        optimizer,
    )

    (
        val_loss,
        val_accuracy,
        _,
        _,
    ) = evaluate(
        model,
        val_loader,
        criterion,
    )

    scheduler.step(val_accuracy)

    history["train_loss"].append(train_loss)
    history["train_accuracy"].append(train_accuracy)

    history["val_loss"].append(val_loss)
    history["val_accuracy"].append(val_accuracy)

    current_lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]

    print(
        f"Epoch [{epoch:02d}/{EPOCHS}] | "
        f"Train Loss: {train_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Train Acc: {train_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"Val Loss: {val_loss:.4f} | "
        f"Val Acc: {val_accuracy * 100:.2f}% | "
        f"LR: {current_lr:.2e}"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SAVE BEST MODEL
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if val_accuracy > best_val_accuracy:

        best_val_accuracy = val_accuracy
        best_epoch = epoch
        patience_counter = 0

        best_model_state = copy.deepcopy(model.state_dict())

        checkpoint = {
            "model_name": "efficientnet_b0",
            "classes": class_names,
            "num_classes": num_classes,
            "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
            "best_val_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
            "model_state_dict": best_model_state,
        }

        torch.save(
            checkpoint,
            BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        )

        print(
            f"  -> Best model saved "
            f"(Val Acc: {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%)"
        )

    else:

        patience_counter += 1

        print(
            f"  -> No improvement "
            f"({patience_counter}/{EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE})"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # EARLY STOPPING
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if patience_counter >= EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE:

        print("\nEarly stopping triggered.")

        break


# ============================================================
# LOAD BEST MODEL
# ============================================================

if best_model_state is not None:

    model.load_state_dict(best_model_state)

else:

    checkpoint = torch.load(
        BEST_MODEL_PATH,
        map_location=DEVICE,
        weights_only=False,
    )

    model.load_state_dict(
        checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL TEST EVALUATION
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("FINAL TEST EVALUATION")
print("=" * 70)

(
    test_loss,
    test_accuracy,
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
) = evaluate(
    model,
    test_loader,
    criterion,
)


print(f"\nTest Loss     : {test_loss:.4f}")
print(f"Test Accuracy : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")


# ============================================================
# CLASSIFICATION REPORT
# ============================================================

report = classification_report(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
    target_names=class_names,
    digits=4,
    zero_division=0,
)

print("\nClassification Report:")
print(report)


# ============================================================
# CONFUSION MATRIX
# ============================================================

cm = confusion_matrix(
    test_labels,
    test_predictions,
)

print("Confusion Matrix:")
print(cm)

with open(
    CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    file.write("Classes:\n")

    for index, name in enumerate(class_names):
        file.write(f"{index}: {name}\n")

    file.write("\nConfusion Matrix:\n")

    for row in cm:
        file.write(" ".join(map(str, row)) + "\n")


# ============================================================
# SAVE TRAINING RESULTS
# ============================================================

results = {
    "model": "EfficientNet-B0",
    "dataset": "pigeonpea_disease_clean",
    "classes": class_names,
    "num_classes": num_classes,
    "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
    "batch_size": BATCH_SIZE,
    "epochs_requested": EPOCHS,
    "best_epoch": best_epoch,
    "best_validation_accuracy": best_val_accuracy,
    "test_loss": test_loss,
    "test_accuracy": test_accuracy,
    "train_samples": len(train_dataset),
    "validation_samples": len(val_dataset),
    "test_samples": len(test_dataset),
    "history": history,
}

with open(
    RESULTS_PATH,
    "w",
    encoding="utf-8",
) as file:

    json.dump(
        results,
        file,
        indent=4,
    )


# ============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ============================================================

print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("TRAINING COMPLETE")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Best validation accuracy : {best_val_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")
print(f"Best epoch              : {best_epoch}")
print(f"Test accuracy           : {test_accuracy * 100:.2f}%")

print(f"\nBest model:")
print(BEST_MODEL_PATH)

print(f"\nTraining results:")
print(RESULTS_PATH)

print(f"\nConfusion matrix:")
print(CONFUSION_MATRIX_PATH)

print("=" * 70)

FILE: backend\alembic\env.py

import sys
from logging.config import fileConfig
from pathlib import Path

from alembic import context
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

# Ensure backend root is in sys.path
backend_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(backend_dir) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(backend_dir))

from app.core.config import settings
from app.database.models import Base  # Loads all models registered on Base

config = context.config

if config.config_file_name:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

target_metadata = Base.metadata

# Override sqlalchemy.url with dynamic DATABASE_URL from core config / env
# configparser treats '%' as an interpolation character, so we escape it by doubling.
_db_url_escaped = settings.DATABASE_URL.replace("%", "%%")
config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", _db_url_escaped)


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    url = config.get_main_option("sqlalchemy.url")
    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
    )

    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    connectable = engine_from_config(
        config.get_section(config.config_ini_section, {}),
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )

    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(
            connection=connection,
            target_metadata=target_metadata,
        )

        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()

================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\api\v1\auth.py

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Header, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.core.security import create_access_token, decode_access_token
from app.database.models.user import User
from app.database.session import get_db
from app.schemas.auth import Token, UserLogin, UserRegister, UserResponse
from app.services.auth_service import authenticate_user, register_user

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["Authentication"])


def get_current_user(
    authorization: str = Header(..., description="Bearer token in format: Bearer <token>"),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> User:
    """
    Dependency that validates JWT access token from request header
    and retrieves current authenticated user.
    """
    if not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Invalid authorization header format. Expected 'Bearer <token>'.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    token = authorization.split(" ")[1].strip()
    payload = decode_access_token(token)
    if not payload or "sub" not in payload:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Invalid or expired access token.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    user_id = payload["sub"]
    try:
        user_id_int = int(user_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Invalid token subject.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id_int).first()
    if not user or not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="User not found or inactive.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    return user


@router.post("/register", response_model=Token, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def register(
    user_in: UserRegister,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Registers a new farmer account.
    Returns signed JWT access token and public user profile upon successful registration.
    """
    new_user = register_user(db=db, user_in=user_in)
    access_token = create_access_token(subject=new_user.id)
    return Token(
        access_token=access_token,
        token_type="bearer",
        user=UserResponse.model_validate(new_user)
    )


@router.post("/login", response_model=Token, status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
def login(
    login_in: UserLogin,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Authenticates a farmer using mobile number OR email and password.
    Returns signed JWT access token and user profile.
    """
    user = authenticate_user(
        db=db,
        identifier=login_in.identifier,
        password=login_in.password
    )
    access_token = create_access_token(subject=user.id)
    return Token(
        access_token=access_token,
        token_type="bearer",
        user=UserResponse.model_validate(user)
    )


@router.get("/me", response_model=UserResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK)
def get_me(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)
):
    """
    Returns the currently authenticated farmer's user profile.
    """
    return UserResponse.model_validate(current_user)


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\api\v1\predictions.py
================================================================================

"""
Predictions API — POST /api/v1/predictions/tur-leaf/{scan_id}

Endpoint:
    POST /api/v1/predictions/tur-leaf/{scan_id}

    Uses the scan's already-stored image (no re-upload required).
    Calls the TurDiseasePredictor via PredictionService.

    When ML model is not configured:
        HTTP 503
        {"status": "model_not_configured", "message": "..."}

    When ML model is available and succeeds:
        HTTP 200
        {"scan_id": 123, "crop": "tur", "disease_name": "...", "confidence": 0.92, ...}

Security:
    - Requires Bearer JWT.
    - Ownership enforced at the service layer.
    - No internal paths in response.
"""

import logging

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.api.v1.auth import get_current_user
from app.database.models.user import User
from app.database.session import get_db
from app.schemas.prediction import PredictionResponse
from app.schemas.claude_guidance import ClaudeGuidanceResponse
from app.services.prediction_service import run_prediction
from app.services.scan_service import get_scan_for_user
from app.services.claude_guidance_service import generate_claude_guidance

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.api.predictions")

router = APIRouter(prefix="/predictions", tags=["Predictions"])


@router.post(
    "/tur-leaf/{scan_id}",
    response_model=PredictionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    summary="Run Tur leaf disease prediction on a stored scan",
)
def predict_tur_leaf(
    scan_id: int,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> PredictionResponse:
    """
    Runs the Tur leaf disease ML prediction on the scan image that was already
    uploaded and stored during the initial scan upload (POST /api/v1/scans).

    The mobile app should NOT re-upload the image — this endpoint uses the
    server-side stored copy identified by scan_id.

    Behaviour when ML model is not yet configured:
        Returns HTTP 503 with:
        {"status": "model_not_configured", "message": "..."}
        The scan record and image are NOT deleted.

    Behaviour when ML model is available:
        Returns HTTP 200 with disease_name, confidence, severity.
        Results are persisted to TurScan.disease_name / .confidence / .severity.
    """
    logger.info(
        "[API/PREDICTIONS] Prediction requested: scan_id=%d user_id=%d",
        scan_id, current_user.id,
    )

    # run_prediction raises HTTPException for model_not_configured (503) or errors
    scan = run_prediction(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=current_user.id)

    return PredictionResponse(
        scan_id=scan.id,
        crop=scan.crop,
        status=scan.prediction_status,
        disease_name=scan.disease_name,
        confidence=scan.confidence,
        severity=scan.severity,
    )


@router.post(
    "/tur-leaf/{scan_id}/ai-guidance",
    response_model=ClaudeGuidanceResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    summary="Get secondary AI guidance and explanation layer powered by Claude Sonnet 4.5",
)
def get_ai_guidance_for_tur_scan(
    scan_id: int,
    language: str = Query("en", description="Target language code: en | mr | hi"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> ClaudeGuidanceResponse:
    """
    Retrieves secondary AI guidance for a Tur scan using Claude Sonnet 4.5.

    - Authenticates the user and validates scan ownership.
    - Uses existing ML prediction (or runs inference if pending).
    - Enforces confidence threshold (>= 60%).
    - Enforces Healthy safety (no pesticide recommendations for healthy leaves).
    - Uses verified recommendation database as the single source of truth.
    - Returns structured AI explanation or database fallback.
    """
    logger.info(
        "[API/PREDICTIONS] AI Guidance requested: scan_id=%d user_id=%d language=%s",
        scan_id, current_user.id, language,
    )

    # 1. Verify scan ownership
    scan = get_scan_for_user(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=current_user.id)

    # 2. Ensure prediction is run
    if scan.prediction_status != "completed" or not scan.disease_name:
        logger.info("[API/PREDICTIONS] Scan #%d prediction not yet completed. Running prediction now...", scan_id)
        scan = run_prediction(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=current_user.id)

    # 3. Generate Claude guidance
    return generate_claude_guidance(db=db, scan=scan, language=language)

﻿import logging
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.api.v1.auth import get_current_user
from app.database.models.user import User
from app.database.session import get_db
from app.schemas.recommendation import SprayGuidanceResponse
from app.services.recommendation_service import get_tur_spray_guidance_service

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.api.recommendations")

router = APIRouter(prefix="/recommendations", tags=["Recommendations"])


@router.get(
    "/tur/{disease_name}/spray-guidance",
    response_model=SprayGuidanceResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    summary="Get verified smart spray guidance for detected Tur disease",
)
def get_tur_spray_guidance(
    disease_name: str,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> SprayGuidanceResponse:
    """
    Fetches verified agricultural spray guidance for a specified Tur disease.

    - Restricts crop to "tur".
    - Queries verified active recommendations from tur_disease_recommendations.
    - Returns HTTP 404 when verified guidance is not available yet.
    - Never fabricates or invents unverified pesticide data.
    """
    clean_name = disease_name.strip()
    if not clean_name:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="Disease name parameter is required.",
        )

    logger.info(
        "[API/RECOMMENDATIONS] Spray guidance requested: disease_name=%s user_id=%d",
        clean_name, current_user.id,
    )

    result = get_tur_spray_guidance_service(db=db, disease_name=clean_name)
    if not result:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Verified spray guidance is not available for this condition yet.",
        )

    return result

FILE: backend\app\api\v1\scans.py
