"""
Scans API — POST /api/v1/scans and POST /api/v1/scans/{scan_id}/voice

Endpoints:
    POST /api/v1/scans
        Receives multipart/form-data with:
            - image  (file upload)
            - crop   (form field, default "tur")
        Returns ScanCreateResponse with scan_id.

    GET /api/v1/scans/{scan_id}
        Returns full scan detail for the authenticated user.

    POST /api/v1/scans/{scan_id}/voice
        Receives multipart/form-data with:
            - audio    (file upload)
            - language (form field, optional BCP-47 code)
            - duration (form field, optional float seconds)
        Returns VoiceReportCreateResponse.

Security:
    - Requires Bearer JWT on every endpoint.
    - Ownership enforced at the service layer.
    - Image and audio validated before write.
    - Server-side UUID filenames prevent path traversal.
    - No internal paths exposed in responses.

Rate-limiting is expected to be handled at the infra level (nginx / API gateway).
"""

import logging
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, Form, HTTPException, UploadFile, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.api.v1.auth import get_current_user
from app.database.models.user import User
from app.database.session import get_db
from app.schemas.scan import ScanCreateResponse, ScanDetailResponse, VoiceReportCreateResponse
from app.services.scan_service import create_scan_with_image, create_voice_report, get_scan_for_user

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.api.scans")

router = APIRouter(prefix="/scans", tags=["Scans"])

# ── Maximum read size guard (secondary, before storage_service validates) ──────
_MAX_IMAGE_READ_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024 + 1  # 20 MB + 1 byte
_MAX_AUDIO_READ_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024 + 1  # 50 MB + 1 byte


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=ScanCreateResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Upload Tur leaf image and create a scan record",
)
async def upload_scan(
    image: UploadFile = File(..., description="Tur leaf image (JPEG, PNG, WebP, HEIC)"),
    crop: str = Form(default="tur", description="Crop type (always 'tur' currently)"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> ScanCreateResponse:
    """
    Accepts a Tur leaf image upload from the mobile app.

    - Validates file type and size.
    - Stores image on server filesystem with a safe UUID filename.
    - Creates a TurScan record in PostgreSQL linked to the authenticated user.
    - Returns scan_id immediately for the mobile app to carry forward.

    The mobile app must pass scan_id to all subsequent screens so that
    voice recordings and future ML results are linked to the correct scan.
    """
    if not image.filename and not image.size:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="No image file was provided.",
        )

    # Read file bytes with size guard
    try:
        file_bytes = await image.read(_MAX_IMAGE_READ_BYTES)
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="Failed to read the uploaded image. Please try again.",
        )

    content_type = image.content_type or "application/octet-stream"
    original_filename = image.filename or "tur_leaf.jpg"

    logger.info(
        "[API/SCANS] Upload received: user_id=%d filename=%s content_type=%s size=%d crop=%s",
        current_user.id, original_filename, content_type, len(file_bytes), crop,
    )

    scan = create_scan_with_image(
        db=db,
        user_id=current_user.id,
        file_bytes=file_bytes,
        content_type=content_type,
        original_filename=original_filename,
        crop=crop,
    )

    return ScanCreateResponse(
        scan_id=scan.id,
        crop=scan.crop,
        status=scan.status,
    )


@router.get(
    "/{scan_id}",
    response_model=ScanDetailResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    summary="Get full scan detail for the authenticated user",
)
def get_scan(
    scan_id: int,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> ScanDetailResponse:
    """
    Returns the full TurScan record for the given scan_id.
    Enforces ownership — only the owner can retrieve their scan.
    """
    scan = get_scan_for_user(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=current_user.id)

    return ScanDetailResponse(
        scan_id=scan.id,
        user_id=scan.user_id,
        crop=scan.crop,
        status=scan.status,
        image_filename=scan.image_filename,
        image_content_type=scan.image_content_type,
        image_size=scan.image_size,
        disease_name=scan.disease_name,
        confidence=scan.confidence,
        severity=scan.severity,
        prediction_status=scan.prediction_status,
        created_at=scan.created_at,
        updated_at=scan.updated_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/{scan_id}/voice",
    response_model=VoiceReportCreateResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Upload farmer voice recording linked to a scan",
)
async def upload_voice_report(
    scan_id: int,
    audio: UploadFile = File(..., description="Farmer voice recording (MP3, M4A, WAV, etc.)"),
    language: Optional[str] = Form(default=None, description="BCP-47 language code: en | mr | hi"),
    duration: Optional[float] = Form(default=None, description="Recording duration in seconds"),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> VoiceReportCreateResponse:
    """
    Receives and stores a farmer's voice recording linked to the given scan.

    - Verifies scan ownership before accepting the upload.
    - Validates audio file type and size.
    - Stores audio on server filesystem with a safe UUID filename.
    - Creates a TurVoiceReport record linked to the scan and user.
    - transcription is null — reserved for future speech-to-text integration.
    """
    if not audio.filename and not audio.size:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="No audio file was provided.",
        )

    try:
        file_bytes = await audio.read(_MAX_AUDIO_READ_BYTES)
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="Failed to read the uploaded audio. Please try again.",
        )

    content_type = audio.content_type or "audio/mp4"
    original_filename = audio.filename or "voice_report.m4a"

    # Normalize language code
    norm_language: Optional[str] = None
    if language:
        lang = language.strip().lower()
        if lang in ("en", "mr", "hi"):
            norm_language = lang

    logger.info(
        "[API/SCANS] Voice upload received: scan_id=%d user_id=%d language=%s size=%d",
        scan_id, current_user.id, norm_language, len(file_bytes),
    )

    voice_report = create_voice_report(
        db=db,
        scan_id=scan_id,
        user_id=current_user.id,
        file_bytes=file_bytes,
        content_type=content_type,
        original_filename=original_filename,
        language=norm_language,
        duration=duration,
    )

    return VoiceReportCreateResponse(
        voice_report_id=voice_report.id,
        scan_id=voice_report.scan_id,
        language=voice_report.language,
        status="received",
    )

import os
from typing import Optional
from urllib.parse import quote_plus
from pydantic import computed_field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    PROJECT_NAME: str = "TurSathi AI Backend"
    API_V1_STR: str = "/api/v1"
    ENVIRONMENT: str = "development"
    DEBUG: bool = True

    # Security & JWT Configuration
    SECRET_KEY: str = "tursathi_ai_super_secret_jwt_key_2026_production_ready"
    ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 60 * 24 * 7  # 7 days

    # PostgreSQL Database Configuration
    POSTGRES_SERVER: str = "127.0.0.1"
    POSTGRES_PORT: int = 5432
    POSTGRES_USER: str = "postgres"
    POSTGRES_PASSWORD: str = "postgres123"
    POSTGRES_DB: str = "tursathi_db"

    # Optional direct DATABASE_URL override
    SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI: Optional[str] = None

    # Anthropic Claude Configuration
    ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: Optional[str] = None
    ANTHROPIC_MODEL: str = "claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929"

    @computed_field
    @property
    def DATABASE_URL(self) -> str:
        if self.SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI:
            return self.SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI
        encoded_password = quote_plus(self.POSTGRES_PASSWORD)
        return (
            f"postgresql://{self.POSTGRES_USER}:{encoded_password}"
            f"@{self.POSTGRES_SERVER}:{self.POSTGRES_PORT}/{self.POSTGRES_DB}"
        )

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__))), ".env"),
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",
    )


settings = Settings()


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\core\security.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any, Optional, Union
import bcrypt
import jwt
from app.core.config import settings


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    """
    Verifies a plain-text password against a stored bcrypt password hash.
    Never exposes passwords or hashes in logs.
    """
    if not plain_password or not hashed_password:
        return False
    try:
        return bcrypt.checkpw(
            plain_password.encode('utf-8'),
            hashed_password.encode('utf-8')
        )
    except Exception:
        return False


def get_password_hash(password: str) -> str:
    """
    Generates a secure 1-way bcrypt password hash.
    Never returns or logs plain-text passwords.
    """
    salt = bcrypt.gensalt()
    return bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode('utf-8'), salt).decode('utf-8')


def create_access_token(
    subject: Union[str, int],
    expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None
) -> str:
    """
    Creates a signed JWT access token containing subject identifier and expiration.
    """
    if expires_delta:
        expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + expires_delta
    else:
        expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    
    to_encode = {
        "sub": str(subject),
        "exp": expire,
        "iat": datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    }
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt


def decode_access_token(token: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Decodes and validates a JWT access token.
    Returns payload dictionary if valid, None if invalid or expired.
    """
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(
            token,
            settings.SECRET_KEY,
            algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM]
        )
        return payload
    except Exception:
        return None

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from app.database.base import Base


class TurDiseaseRecommendation(Base):
    """
    Represents verified agricultural recommendations for detected Tur crop diseases.

    Used by the Treatment Recommendation and Smart Spray Guidance pages
    to map ML-detected disease names to verified treatment guidance.
    """

    __tablename__ = "tur_disease_recommendations"

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "crop",
            "disease_name",
            name="uq_tur_disease_recommendations_crop_disease",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)

    # Crop identifier (default "tur")
    crop: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False, default="tur", index=True)

    # ML disease class/name (e.g. "Phytophthora Blight", "Tur Leaf Spot")
    disease_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False, index=True)

    # Short farmer-friendly explanation of the disease
    disease_description: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Verified treatment recommendation text
    treatment: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Active ingredient / chemical or organic formulation details
    active_ingredient: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Recommended quantity (e.g., "500 ml/acre")
    recommended_quantity: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Water quantity (e.g., "200 L/acre")
    water_quantity: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Per pump quantity (e.g., "30 ml per 15L pump")
    per_pump_quantity: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Recommended spray timing (e.g., "Early morning 7-10 AM")
    spray_timing: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Application instructions and nozzle guidance
    application_method: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Safety precautions and protective gear guidance
    safety_precautions: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Post-spray monitoring and follow-up advisory
    after_spray_advisory: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # Status flag allowing recommendations to be toggled without deleting history
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, default=True, index=True)

    # Timestamps
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<TurDiseaseRecommendation id={self.id} crop={self.crop} "
            f"disease_name={self.disease_name} is_active={self.is_active}>"
        )

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, String, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.database.base import Base


class TurScan(Base):
    """
    Represents a single Tur leaf scan/case submitted by a farmer.

    Lifecycle:
        received     → Image uploaded, scan record created.
        processing   → Prediction service is actively running inference.
        completed    → Prediction result saved.
        failed       → Prediction failed after all retries.

    The actual image binary is stored on the filesystem (storage service).
    Only the storage reference (image_path) is persisted in the database.
    """

    __tablename__ = "tur_scans"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)

    # Ownership — every scan belongs to a registered authenticated farmer
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )

    # Crop identifier (always "tur" for this model, reserved for future crops)
    crop: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False, default="tur")

    # Image storage reference (relative path from UPLOAD_DIR root, never absolute)
    image_path: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)

    # Original client-provided filename (sanitized server-side before storage)
    image_filename: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # MIME type as reported during upload (validated server-side)
    image_content_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)

    # File size in bytes
    image_size: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)

    # Overall scan pipeline status
    # Allowed values: received | processing | completed | failed
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(50), nullable=False, default="received", index=True
    )

    # ── ML Prediction results (populated when model is available) ──────────────

    # Human-readable disease name (e.g. "Phytophthora Blight")
    disease_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Model confidence score 0.0 – 1.0
    confidence: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)

    # Severity level string (e.g. "mild", "moderate", "severe")
    severity: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)

    # ML prediction pipeline status
    # Allowed values: pending | processing | completed | failed | model_not_configured
    prediction_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(50), nullable=False, default="pending"
    )

    # ── Timestamps ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # ── Relationships ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    # Back-reference to voice reports linked to this scan
    voice_reports: Mapped[list["TurVoiceReport"]] = relationship(  # noqa: F821
        "TurVoiceReport",
        back_populates="scan",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        lazy="select",
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<TurScan id={self.id} user_id={self.user_id} "
            f"crop={self.crop} status={self.status} "
            f"prediction_status={self.prediction_status}>"
        )


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\database\models\tur_voice_report.py

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, String, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.database.base import Base


class TurVoiceReport(Base):
    """
    Represents a farmer's voice description linked to a specific TurScan case.

    The audio binary is stored on the filesystem (storage service).
    Only the storage reference (audio_path) and metadata are persisted here.
    Transcription is nullable — reserved for future speech-to-text integration.
    """

    __tablename__ = "tur_voice_reports"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)

    # Link to parent scan — one scan can have one or more voice reports
    scan_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        ForeignKey("tur_scans.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )

    # Denormalized for query convenience (avoids join to tur_scans for ownership checks)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )

    # Storage reference (relative path from UPLOAD_DIR root)
    audio_path: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)

    # Original client-provided filename (sanitized server-side)
    audio_filename: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # MIME type (e.g. "audio/mpeg", "audio/mp4", "audio/wav")
    audio_content_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)

    # File size in bytes
    audio_size: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)

    # BCP-47 language tag derived from farmer's active language selection
    # Supported: "en" | "mr" | "hi"
    language: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)

    # Recording duration in seconds (client-reported, not authoritative)
    duration: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)

    # Transcription text — null until speech-to-text pipeline is implemented
    transcription: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8192), nullable=True)

    # ── Timestamps ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # ── Relationship ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    scan: Mapped["TurScan"] = relationship(  # noqa: F821
        "TurScan",
        back_populates="voice_reports",
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"<TurVoiceReport id={self.id} scan_id={self.scan_id} "
            f"user_id={self.user_id} language={self.language}>"
        )

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, String, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from app.database.base import Base


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    full_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    mobile_number: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    email: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), unique=True, index=True, nullable=True)
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        nullable=False
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
        nullable=False
    )

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<User id={self.id} mobile={self.mobile_number} full_name={self.full_name}>"

from typing import Generator
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, Session
from app.core.config import settings

# Create SQLAlchemy Engine for PostgreSQL with connection pooling
engine = create_engine(
    settings.DATABASE_URL,
    pool_pre_ping=True,
    pool_size=10,
    max_overflow=20,
    echo=settings.DEBUG,
)

# Session factory bound to engine
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(
    autocommit=False,
    autoflush=False,
    bind=engine,
)


def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    """
    Dependency generator yielding a database session per request.
    Ensures proper cleanup and closure after handling requests.
    """
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

import time
import logging
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from app.api.v1 import api_router
from app.core.config import settings

# Configure logging to output request details directly to the terminal
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.api")

app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.PROJECT_NAME,
    version="1.0.0",
    openapi_url=f"{settings.API_V1_STR}/openapi.json",
    docs_url=f"{settings.API_V1_STR}/docs",
    redoc_url=f"{settings.API_V1_STR}/redoc",
)

@app.middleware("http")
async def log_incoming_requests(request: Request, call_next):
    start_time = time.time()
    client_ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
    method = request.method
    path = request.url.path
    origin = request.headers.get("origin", "no-origin")
    
    print(f"\n==================================================")
    print(f"[INCOMING API REQUEST] {method} {path}")
    print(f" -> Client IP: {client_ip}")
    print(f" -> Origin: {origin}")
    print(f"==================================================")
    
    response = await call_next(request)
    
    duration = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
    print(f"[API RESPONSE] {method} {path} -> Status: {response.status_code} ({duration:.2f}ms)\n")
    return response

# CORS Configuration for Mobile / Web clients
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://localhost:8081",
        "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
        "http://localhost:8000",
        "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        "*",
    ],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Include API v1 Router
app.include_router(api_router, prefix=settings.API_V1_STR)

        "success": True,
        "name": settings.PROJECT_NAME,
        "status": "online",
        "environment": settings.ENVIRONMENT,
    }

    return {
        "status": "healthy",
        "database": "connected"
    }

# ml package init


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\ml\tur_predictor.py
================================================================================

"""
Real EfficientNet-B0 predictor for Tur leaf disease classification.

Flow:
    API route
        ↓
    PredictionService
        ↓
    TurDiseasePredictor
        ↓
    EfficientNet-B0
        ↓
    Disease + confidence

Supported classes:
    Healthy
    Leaf_Spot
    Leaf_webber
    Sterilic_mosaic
"""

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from PIL import Image
from torchvision import models, transforms


logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.ml")


# ============================================================
# PATHS
# ============================================================

# backend/app/ml/tur_predictor.py
# parents[0] = ml
# parents[1] = app
# parents[2] = backend
# parents[3] = TurSathi AI project root

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]

MODEL_PATH = (
    PROJECT_ROOT
    / "models"
    / "best_pigeonpea_disease_final_efficientnet_b0.pth"
)


# ============================================================
# DEVICE
# ============================================================

DEVICE = torch.device(
    "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
)


# ============================================================
# MODEL FEATURE FLAG
# ============================================================

MODEL_AVAILABLE: bool = MODEL_PATH.exists()


# ============================================================
# RESULT
# ============================================================

@dataclass
class PredictionResult:
    """
    Structured result from the Tur disease ML model.
    """

    disease_name: str
    confidence: float
    severity: str
    prediction_status: str = "completed"


# ============================================================
# EXCEPTION
# ============================================================

class ModelNotConfiguredError(Exception):
    """
    Raised when the Tur disease ML model is not configured.
    """

    pass


# ============================================================
# PREDICTOR
# ============================================================

class TurDiseasePredictor:
    """
    EfficientNet-B0 based Tur leaf disease predictor.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:

        self._model = None

        self._classes: list[str] = []

        self._transform = transforms.Compose([
            transforms.Resize((224, 224)),

            transforms.ToTensor(),

            transforms.Normalize(
                mean=[
                    0.485,
                    0.456,
                    0.406,
                ],
                std=[
                    0.229,
                    0.224,
                    0.225,
                ],
            ),
        ])

        if not MODEL_AVAILABLE:

            logger.warning(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                f"Model file not found: {MODEL_PATH}"
            )

            return

        self._load_model()


    # ========================================================
    # LOAD MODEL
    # ========================================================

    def _load_model(self) -> None:
        """
        Load the trained EfficientNet-B0 checkpoint.
        """

        logger.info(
            "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
            f"Loading model from: {MODEL_PATH}"
        )

        if not MODEL_PATH.exists():

            raise ModelNotConfiguredError(
                "Tur disease ML model file was not found."
            )

        try:

            checkpoint = torch.load(
                MODEL_PATH,
                map_location=DEVICE,
                weights_only=False,
            )

            classes = checkpoint.get("classes")

            if not classes:

                raise ModelNotConfiguredError(
                    "Model checkpoint does not contain class information."
                )

            self._classes = list(classes)

            num_classes = len(
                self._classes
            )

            # -----------------------------------------------
            # Create EfficientNet-B0 architecture
            # -----------------------------------------------

            model = models.efficientnet_b0(
                weights=None
            )

            in_features = (
                model.classifier[1].in_features
            )

            model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(
                in_features,
                num_classes,
            )

            # -----------------------------------------------
            # Load trained weights
            # -----------------------------------------------

            model.load_state_dict(
                checkpoint["model_state_dict"]
            )

            model = model.to(
                DEVICE
            )

            model.eval()

            self._model = model

            logger.info(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                "Model loaded successfully."
            )

            logger.info(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                f"Device: {DEVICE}"
            )

            logger.info(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                f"Classes: {self._classes}"
            )

            if torch.cuda.is_available():

                logger.info(
                    "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                    f"GPU: "
                    f"{torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}"
                )

        except ModelNotConfiguredError:

            raise

        except Exception as exc:

            logger.exception(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                "Failed to load model."
            )

            raise ModelNotConfiguredError(
                "Tur disease ML model could not be loaded."
            ) from exc


    # ========================================================
    # AVAILABILITY
    # ========================================================

    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        """
        Returns True when the model is loaded and ready.
        """

        return (
            MODEL_AVAILABLE
            and self._model is not None
        )


    # ========================================================
    # SEVERITY
    # ========================================================

    @staticmethod
    def _map_severity(
        confidence: float,
    ) -> str:
        """
        The trained classifier predicts disease class and
        confidence, not biological disease severity.

        Until a separately validated severity model/rule is
        available, keep severity conservative.
        """

        if confidence >= 0.90:
            return "high_confidence"

        if confidence >= 0.70:
            return "moderate_confidence"

        return "low_confidence"


    # ========================================================
    # PREDICT
    # ========================================================

    def predict(
        self,
        image_path: Path,
    ) -> PredictionResult:
        """
        Run EfficientNet-B0 inference on a stored scan image.
        """

        if not self.is_available():

            raise ModelNotConfiguredError(
                "Tur disease ML model is not configured yet."
            )

        if not image_path.exists():

            raise FileNotFoundError(
                "Scan image not found at expected path. "
                "Cannot run prediction."
            )

        try:

            # -----------------------------------------------
            # Load image
            # -----------------------------------------------

            image = Image.open(
                image_path
            ).convert("RGB")

            # -----------------------------------------------
            # Transform image
            # -----------------------------------------------

            input_tensor = self._transform(
                image
            ).unsqueeze(0)

            input_tensor = input_tensor.to(
                DEVICE
            )

            # -----------------------------------------------
            # Inference
            # -----------------------------------------------

            with torch.no_grad():

                output = self._model(
                    input_tensor
                )

                probabilities = torch.softmax(
                    output,
                    dim=1,
                )[0]

                predicted_index = (
                    probabilities.argmax().item()
                )

                confidence = (
                    probabilities[
                        predicted_index
                    ].item()
                )

            disease_name = self._classes[
                predicted_index
            ]

            severity = self._map_severity(
                confidence
            )

            logger.info(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                f"Prediction: {disease_name} "
                f"confidence={confidence:.4f}"
            )

            return PredictionResult(
                disease_name=disease_name,
                confidence=confidence,
                severity=severity,
                prediction_status="completed",
            )

        except FileNotFoundError:

            raise

        except Exception as exc:

            logger.exception(
                "[TurDiseasePredictor] "
                "Prediction failed."
            )

            raise ValueError(
                "The scan image could not be processed "
                "for disease prediction."
            ) from exc


# ============================================================
# MODULE-LEVEL SINGLETON
# ============================================================

_predictor_instance: Optional[
    TurDiseasePredictor
] = None


def get_predictor() -> TurDiseasePredictor:
    """
    Returns the module-level predictor singleton.
    """

    global _predictor_instance

    if _predictor_instance is None:

        _predictor_instance = (
            TurDiseasePredictor()
        )

    return _predictor_instance

================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\schemas\auth.py

from datetime import datetime
import re
from typing import Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator


class UserRegister(BaseModel):
    full_name: str = Field(..., description="User's full name")
    mobile_number: str = Field(..., description="10-digit Indian mobile number")
    email: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Optional email address")
    password: str = Field(..., min_length=8, description="Password (at least 8 characters)")
    confirm_password: str = Field(..., description="Must match password")

    @field_validator("full_name")
    @classmethod
    def validate_full_name(cls, v: str) -> str:
        trimmed = v.strip()
        if not trimmed:
            raise ValueError("Full name cannot be empty")
        return trimmed

    @field_validator("mobile_number")
    @classmethod
    def validate_mobile_number(cls, v: str) -> str:
        # Strip spaces, hyphens, and +91 prefix
        cleaned = re.sub(r"[\s()\-\+]", "", v)
        if cleaned.startswith("91") and len(cleaned) == 12:
            cleaned = cleaned[2:]
        
        if not re.match(r"^[6-9]\d{9}$", cleaned):
            raise ValueError("Mobile number must be a valid 10-digit Indian mobile number")
        return cleaned

    @field_validator("email")
    @classmethod
    def validate_email(cls, v: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
        if v is None:
            return None
        trimmed = v.strip().lower()
        if not trimmed:
            return None
        if not re.match(r"^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$", trimmed):
            raise ValueError("Enter a valid email address or leave it blank")
        return trimmed

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_passwords_match(self) -> "UserRegister":
        if self.password != self.confirm_password:
            raise ValueError("Password and Confirm Password do not match")
        return self


class UserLogin(BaseModel):
    identifier: str = Field(..., description="Mobile number or Email address")
    password: str = Field(..., description="User's password")

    @field_validator("identifier")
    @classmethod
    def validate_identifier(cls, v: str) -> str:
        trimmed = v.strip()
        if not trimmed:
            raise ValueError("Mobile number or Email address is required")
        return trimmed

    @field_validator("password")
    @classmethod
    def validate_password(cls, v: str) -> str:
        if not v:
            raise ValueError("Password is required")
        return v


class UserResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    full_name: str
    mobile_number: str
    email: Optional[str] = None
    is_active: bool
    created_at: datetime

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)


class Token(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"
    user: UserResponse

    warnings: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Safety warnings or low-confidence notes")
    language: str = Field(default="en", description="Language of human-readable guidance (en, mr, hi)")
    important_alert: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Override alert message for special cases (e.g., seed treatment instead of foliar spray)")

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

from typing import Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class PredictionResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    Response schema for the prediction endpoint.

    When the ML model is not yet configured the HTTP status is 503
    and status field is "model_not_configured".

    When the real model is available:
        status = "completed"
        disease_name, confidence, severity are populated.
    """
    scan_id: int
    crop: str
    status: str  # model_not_configured | pending | processing | completed | failed
    message: Optional[str] = None

    # Populated only when prediction_status == "completed"
    disease_name: Optional[str] = None
    confidence: Optional[float] = None
    severity: Optional[str] = None

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

from app.services.auth_service import authenticate_user, register_user

__all__ = ["register_user", "authenticate_user"]


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\auth_service.py
================================================================================

import re
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy import or_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.core.security import get_password_hash, verify_password
from app.database.models.user import User
from app.schemas.auth import UserRegister


def register_user(db: Session, user_in: UserRegister) -> User:
    """
    Registers a new user after verifying duplicate mobile number and email.
    Hashes the password securely and commits the user to PostgreSQL.
    """
    # 1. Check duplicate mobile number
    existing_mobile = db.query(User).filter(User.mobile_number == user_in.mobile_number).first()
    if existing_mobile:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="Mobile number is already registered. Please login or use a different mobile number."
        )

    # 2. Check duplicate email if provided
    if user_in.email:
        existing_email = db.query(User).filter(User.email == user_in.email).first()
        if existing_email:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="Email address is already registered. Please login or use a different email."
            )

    # 3. Hash password securely
    hashed_password = get_password_hash(user_in.password)

    # 4. Create User instance
    db_user = User(
        full_name=user_in.full_name,
        mobile_number=user_in.mobile_number,
        email=user_in.email,
        password_hash=hashed_password,
        is_active=True
    )

    db.add(db_user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_user)
    return db_user


def authenticate_user(db: Session, identifier: str, password: str) -> User:
    """
    Authenticates a user by email OR mobile number and verifies password.
    Returns user instance or raises HTTP 401/400.
    """
    cleaned_identifier = identifier.strip()
    
    # Normalize mobile number if identifier looks like mobile
    cleaned_mobile = re.sub(r"[\s()\-\+]", "", cleaned_identifier)
    if cleaned_mobile.startswith("91") and len(cleaned_mobile) == 12:
        cleaned_mobile = cleaned_mobile[2:]

    # Query user by mobile number OR email
    db_user = db.query(User).filter(
        or_(
            User.mobile_number == cleaned_mobile,
            User.mobile_number == cleaned_identifier,
            User.email == cleaned_identifier.lower()
        )
    ).first()

    if not db_user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Invalid mobile number/email or password.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    if not verify_password(password, db_user.password_hash):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Invalid mobile number/email or password.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    if not db_user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="User account is inactive. Please contact support."
        )

    return db_user


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\claude_guidance_service.py
================================================================================

def _get_lang_key(language: str) -> str:
    lang = (language or "en").strip().lower()
    return lang if lang in ("en", "mr", "hi") else "en"


def _is_seed_treatment(rec: Optional[TurDiseaseRecommendation]) -> bool:
    """Returns True if the recommendation is a seed treatment (not a foliar spray)."""
    if not rec:
        return False
    app = (rec.application_method or "").lower()
    return "seed treatment" in app or "seed treat" in app


# Localized important alerts for seed treatment (not a foliar spray)
SEED_TREATMENT_ALERT: Dict[str, str] = {
    "en": "This is a SEED TREATMENT — apply before sowing, not as a foliar spray. Trichoderma viride is a biological agent. Follow the product label instructions.",
    "mr": "हे बियाणे उपचार आहे — पेरणीपूर्वी वापरावे, पान फवारणी नाही. Trichoderma viride एक जैविक बुरशीनाशक आहे. उत्पादनाच्या label वरील सूचनांचे पालन करा.",
    "hi": "यह बीज उपचार है — बुवाई से पहले उपयोग करें, पत्तियों पर छिड़काव नहीं। Trichoderma viride एक जैविक फफूंदनाशक है। उत्पाद के label पर दिए निर्देशों का पालन करें।",
}


def _build_fallback_guidance(
    scan: TurScan,
    rec: Optional[TurDiseaseRecommendation],
    language: str,
    warnings: List[str]
) -> ClaudeGuidanceResponse:
    lang_key = _get_lang_key(language)
    conf_pct = int(round((scan.confidence or 0.0) * 100)) if scan.confidence is not None else 0

    if not rec:
        return ClaudeGuidanceResponse(
            status="fallback",
            crop="tur",
            disease_name=scan.disease_name,
            confidence=scan.confidence,
            confidence_percent=conf_pct,
            disease_explanation=f"Detected disease: {scan.disease_name}. Verified spray guidance is currently unavailable for this disease.",
            farmer_action=["Monitor crop condition closely.", "Please consult a qualified agricultural expert before applying any pesticide."],
            spray_guidance_available=False,
            ai_guidance_available=True,
            spray_guidance=None,
            warnings=warnings + ["Verified spray guidance is currently unavailable for this disease."],
            language=lang_key,
        )

    is_seed = _is_seed_treatment(rec)

    verified_details = SprayGuidanceDetails(
        treatment=rec.treatment,
        active_ingredient=rec.active_ingredient,
        recommended_quantity=rec.recommended_quantity,
        water_quantity=rec.water_quantity,
        per_pump_quantity=rec.per_pump_quantity,
        spray_timing=rec.spray_timing,
        application_method=rec.application_method,
        safety_precautions=rec.safety_precautions,
        after_spray_advisory=rec.after_spray_advisory,
    )

    if lang_key == "mr":
        explanation = f"आढळलेला रोग: {rec.disease_name}. {rec.disease_description or ''}"
        if is_seed:
            actions = [
                f"प्रमाणित बियाणे उपचार वापरा: {rec.treatment or 'कृषी सल्लागाराचा सल्ला घ्या'}.",
                f"प्रमाण: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'लेबलनुसार'}.",
                "बियाणे उपचारानंतर सावलीत वाळवा, उन्हात नाही.",
                "उपचारित बियाणे हाताळताना हात धुवा."
            ]
        else:
            actions = [
                f"प्रमाणित उपचार वापरा: {rec.treatment or 'कृषी सल्लागाराचा सल्ला घ्या'}.",
                f"प्रमाण: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'लेबलनुसार'}.",
                "फवारणी करताना योग्य सुरक्षा साधनांचा वापर करा."
            ]
    elif lang_key == "hi":
        explanation = f"पहचाना गया रोग: {rec.disease_name}। {rec.disease_description or ''}"
        if is_seed:
            actions = [
                f"सत्यापित बीज उपचार: {rec.treatment or 'कृषि अधिकारी की सलाह लें'}।",
                f"मात्रा: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'लेबल के अनुसार'}।",
                "बीज उपचार के बाद छाया में सुखाएं, धूप में नहीं।",
                "उपचारित बीज को संभालने के बाद हाथ धोएं।"
            ]
        else:
            actions = [
                f"सत्यापित उपचार: {rec.treatment or 'कृषि अधिकारी की सलाह लें'}।",
                f"मात्रा: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'लेबल के अनुसार'}।",
                "छिड़काव के समय सुरक्षात्मक कपड़े पहनें।"
            ]
    else:
        explanation = f"Detected condition: {rec.disease_name}. {rec.disease_description or ''}"
        if is_seed:
            actions = [
                f"Apply verified seed treatment: {rec.treatment or 'Consult agricultural advisor'}.",
                f"Recommended dose: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'As per product label'}.",
                "After treating seeds, shade-dry for ~2 hours before sowing.",
                "Wash hands after handling treated seeds."
            ]
        else:
            actions = [
                f"Apply verified treatment: {rec.treatment or 'Consult agricultural advisor'}.",
                f"Recommended dose: {rec.recommended_quantity or 'As per product label'}.",
                "Follow all safety precautions during spraying."
            ]

    return ClaudeGuidanceResponse(
        status="fallback",
        crop="tur",
        disease_name=rec.disease_name,
        confidence=scan.confidence,
        confidence_percent=conf_pct,
        disease_explanation=explanation.strip(),
        farmer_action=actions,
        spray_guidance_available=True,
        ai_guidance_available=False,
        spray_guidance=verified_details,
        warnings=warnings,
        language=lang_key,
        important_alert=SEED_TREATMENT_ALERT.get(lang_key) if is_seed else None,
    )


def generate_claude_guidance(
    db: Session,
    scan: TurScan,
    language: str = "en"
) -> ClaudeGuidanceResponse:
    """
    Generates AI guidance for a Tur scan using Claude Sonnet 4.5.
    Enforces confidence validation (>= 60%) and database verification rules.
    Falls back gracefully if Claude API key is missing or call fails.
    """
    lang_key = _get_lang_key(language)
    conf_val = scan.confidence or 0.0
    conf_pct = int(round(conf_val * 100)) if scan.confidence is not None else 0

    # 1. Check Confidence Safety (< 60%)
    if conf_val < 0.60:
        logger.info("[CLAUDE GUIDANCE] Scan #%d confidence (%.2f) < 0.60. Returning low confidence response.", scan.id, conf_val)
        low_data = LOW_CONFIDENCE_DATA[lang_key]
        return ClaudeGuidanceResponse(
            status="low_confidence",
            crop="tur",
            disease_name=scan.disease_name,
            confidence=scan.confidence,
            confidence_percent=conf_pct,
            disease_explanation=str(low_data["explanation"]),
            farmer_action=list(low_data["actions"]),
            spray_guidance_available=False,
            ai_guidance_available=True,
            spray_guidance=None,
            warnings=["Low confidence prediction (<60%). Spray guidance disabled for safety."],
            language=lang_key,
        )

    disease_clean = (scan.disease_name or "").strip()

    # 2. Check Healthy Safety
    if disease_clean.lower() == "healthy":
        logger.info("[CLAUDE GUIDANCE] Scan #%d is Healthy. Returning healthy guidance without chemical spray recommendations.", scan.id)
        healthy_data = HEALTHY_DATA[lang_key]
        return ClaudeGuidanceResponse(
            status="success",
            crop="tur",
            disease_name="Healthy",
            confidence=scan.confidence,
            confidence_percent=conf_pct,
            disease_explanation=str(healthy_data["explanation"]),
            farmer_action=list(healthy_data["actions"]),
            spray_guidance_available=False,
            ai_guidance_available=True,
            spray_guidance=None,
            warnings=[],
            language=lang_key,
        )

    # 3. Retrieve Verified Recommendation Record from Database
    query = select(TurDiseaseRecommendation).where(
        TurDiseaseRecommendation.crop == "tur",
        func.lower(TurDiseaseRecommendation.disease_name) == disease_clean.lower(),
        TurDiseaseRecommendation.is_active == True,
    )
    rec = db.scalar(query)

    if not rec:
        logger.warning("[CLAUDE GUIDANCE] No active recommendation in database for disease: %s", disease_clean)
        return _build_fallback_guidance(
            scan=scan,
            rec=None,
            language=lang_key,
            warnings=[f"No verified recommendation in database for disease: {disease_clean}"]
        )

    calculated_pump_qty = rec.per_pump_quantity
    if not calculated_pump_qty and rec.recommended_quantity:
        if rec.disease_name.lower() == "leaf_spot" and "2.5 g/l" in rec.recommended_quantity.lower():
            calculated_pump_qty = "37.5 g"
        elif rec.disease_name.lower() == "sterilic_mosaic":
            calculated_pump_qty = "15 ml"

    verified_details = SprayGuidanceDetails(
        treatment=rec.treatment,
        active_ingredient=rec.active_ingredient,
        recommended_quantity=rec.recommended_quantity,
        water_quantity=rec.water_quantity,  # Preserved as NULL if None
        per_pump_quantity=calculated_pump_qty,  # Preserved as NULL if None
        spray_timing=rec.spray_timing,
        application_method=rec.application_method,
        safety_precautions=rec.safety_precautions,
        after_spray_advisory=rec.after_spray_advisory,
    )

    # 4. Check Anthropic API Key
    api_key = settings.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
    if not api_key or not api_key.strip():
        logger.info("[CLAUDE GUIDANCE] ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not configured. Returning verified database fallback.")
        return _build_fallback_guidance(
            scan=scan,
            rec=rec,
            language=lang_key,
            warnings=["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not configured in backend environment."]
        )

    # 5. Call Anthropic Claude API
    try:
        import anthropic
        client = anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=api_key.strip())

        system_prompt = (
            "You are a translation and explanation layer for Tur (pigeonpea) crop health.\n"
            "Your task is ONLY to simplify and translate the provided verified recommendation data into the requested language.\n\n"
            "STRICT RULES:\n"
            "1. ALL farmer-facing explanatory text MUST be entirely in the requested target_language. DO NOT mix English sentences into Marathi or Hindi text. However, technical product names, chemical names (e.g., Mancozeb 75 WP, Fenazaquin 10 EC), units (e.g., 2.5 g/L), and verified quantities MUST remain unchanged in their original technical format.\n"
            "2. NEVER treat 'confidence' as disease severity (e.g. 67% confidence does NOT mean 'mild' or 'early stage'). Confidence is only the AI's detection certainty. State confidence separately and clearly.\n"
            "3. NEVER infer disease cause (e.g. do not say 'fungal') or biological severity unless explicitly present in the trusted data.\n"
            "4. NEVER invent, calculate, or add pesticide dosage, water quantity, pump quantity, spray techniques (e.g. nozzle, pressure), intervals, or claims (e.g. '100% effective').\n"
            "5. NEVER make guidance sound more certain than the trusted data (e.g. no 'Definitely' or 'Guaranteed').\n"
            "6. In 'disease_explanation', provide a short conservative explanation of the detection. ONLY IF supported by the trusted context, add a short explanation of 'Why this spray' (e.g., if transmitted by mites, explain the spray helps control the mite associated with the disease). Do not make unsupported claims.\n"
            "7. 'farmer_action' must be a short, practical summary using ONLY the verified treatment, quantity, timing, application method, safety, and after-spray advisory. Do not add extra agricultural instructions. ALWAYS include this exact safe mixing warning as the final point: If English: 'Before mixing more than one product, check the product label or verified agricultural recommendation.' If Marathi: 'एकापेक्षा जास्त औषधे एकत्र मिसळण्यापूर्वी उत्पादनाच्या label वरील सूचना किंवा अधिकृत कृषी शिफारस तपासा.' If Hindi: 'एक से अधिक दवाओं को मिलाने से पहले उत्पाद के label या आधिकारिक कृषि सिफारिश की जांच करें।'\n"
            "8. Return ONLY a valid JSON object matching this exact structure with no markdown codeblocks:\n"
            "{\n"
            '  "disease_explanation": "Short conservative explanation in target language (includes confidence and optional verified why-this-spray)",\n'
            '  "farmer_action": ["Action step 1 in target language", "Action step 2 in target language"]\n'
            "}\n"
        )

        user_input_payload = {
            "crop": "tur",
            "disease_name": rec.disease_name,
            "detection_confidence": f"{conf_pct}%",
            "disease_description": rec.disease_description,
            "treatment": rec.treatment,
            "active_ingredient": rec.active_ingredient,
            "recommended_quantity": rec.recommended_quantity,
            "water_quantity": rec.water_quantity,
            "per_pump_quantity": rec.per_pump_quantity,
            "spray_timing": rec.spray_timing,
            "application_method": rec.application_method,
            "safety_precautions": rec.safety_precautions,
            "after_spray_advisory": rec.after_spray_advisory,
            "target_language": lang_key
        }

        user_prompt = (
            f"Here is the verified crop disease data:\n"
            f"{json.dumps(user_input_payload, indent=2)}\n\n"
            f"Generate the disease_explanation and farmer_action in language code '{lang_key}'."
        )

        response = client.messages.create(
            model=settings.ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
            max_tokens=1024,
            system=system_prompt,
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": user_prompt}]
        )

        response_text = ""
        for block in response.content:
            if hasattr(block, "text"):
                response_text += block.text

        # Clean codeblock wrappers if any
        clean_text = response_text.strip()
        if clean_text.startswith("```"):
            clean_text = clean_text.split("\n", 1)[-1]
            if clean_text.endswith("```"):
                clean_text = clean_text[:-3]
            clean_text = clean_text.strip()

        parsed_claude = json.loads(clean_text)
        disease_explanation = parsed_claude.get("disease_explanation")
        farmer_action = parsed_claude.get("farmer_action")

        if not disease_explanation or not isinstance(farmer_action, list):
            raise ValueError("Invalid JSON schema returned by Claude")

        is_seed_treat = _is_seed_treatment(rec)

        return ClaudeGuidanceResponse(
            status="success",
            crop="tur",
            disease_name=rec.disease_name,
            confidence=scan.confidence,
            confidence_percent=conf_pct,
            disease_explanation=str(disease_explanation),
            farmer_action=[str(a) for a in farmer_action],
            spray_guidance_available=True,
            ai_guidance_available=True,
            spray_guidance=verified_details,
            warnings=[],
            language=lang_key,
            important_alert=SEED_TREATMENT_ALERT.get(lang_key) if is_seed_treat else None,
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("[CLAUDE GUIDANCE] Exception during Claude API call: %s", exc)
        return _build_fallback_guidance(
            scan=scan,
            rec=rec,
            language=lang_key,
            warnings=[f"Claude API failed: fallback to verified database guidance."]
        )


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\prediction_service.py
================================================================================

"""
Prediction Service — orchestrates ML inference on stored scan images.

Architecture:
    API route → PredictionService.run_prediction(scan_id, user_id)
             → validates scan ownership
             → retrieves stored image path
             → calls TurDiseasePredictor.predict()
             → persists result back to TurScan record

When the ML model is not configured:
    - TurScan.prediction_status is set to "model_not_configured".
    - The scan record is NOT deleted — the image remains safely stored.
    - The caller (API route) raises HTTP 503.

DO NOT generate fake predictions in this service.
"""

import logging
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database.models.tur_scan import TurScan
from app.ml.tur_predictor import ModelNotConfiguredError, get_predictor
from app.services.scan_service import get_scan_for_user
from app.services.storage_service import UPLOAD_DIR

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.prediction_service")


def run_prediction(
    db: Session,
    scan_id: int,
    user_id: int,
) -> TurScan:
    """
    Runs ML prediction for the given scan.

    Steps:
    1. Retrieves and verifies scan ownership.
    2. Resolves the stored image path.
    3. Calls the ML predictor.
    4. Persists result to TurScan record.
    5. Returns updated TurScan.

    Raises:
        HTTP 404 — scan not found.
        HTTP 403 — scan belongs to another user.
        HTTP 503 — ML model not yet configured (model_not_configured).
        HTTP 500 — unexpected prediction failure.
    """
    # 1. Get scan (enforces ownership)
    scan = get_scan_for_user(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=user_id)

    # 2. Ensure image exists in storage
    if not scan.image_path:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail="Scan does not have an associated image.",
        )

    image_abs_path = (UPLOAD_DIR / scan.image_path).resolve()

    # 3. Mark as processing
    scan.status = "processing"
    scan.prediction_status = "processing"
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(scan)

    # 4. Run predictor
    predictor = get_predictor()
    try:
        result = predictor.predict(image_abs_path)

        # 5a. Persist completed result
        scan.disease_name = result.disease_name
        scan.confidence = result.confidence
        scan.severity = result.severity
        scan.prediction_status = "completed"
        scan.status = "completed"
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(scan)

        logger.info(
            "[PREDICTION_SERVICE] Scan #%d prediction completed: disease=%s confidence=%.3f",
            scan_id, result.disease_name, result.confidence,
        )
        return scan

    except ModelNotConfiguredError:
        # 5b. Model not configured — mark the scan but preserve image and record
        scan.prediction_status = "model_not_configured"
        scan.status = "received"  # Revert to received — image is safe, scan is valid
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(scan)

        logger.warning(
            "[PREDICTION_SERVICE] Scan #%d: ML model not configured. "
            "Image and scan record are safely preserved.",
            scan_id,
        )
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail={
                "status": "model_not_configured",
                "message": "Tur disease ML model is not configured yet.",
            },
        )

    except FileNotFoundError:
        scan.prediction_status = "failed"
        scan.status = "failed"
        db.commit()
        logger.error("[PREDICTION_SERVICE] Scan #%d: image file not found on disk.", scan_id)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Scan image could not be found on the server.",
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        # Unexpected error — mark failed but preserve the record
        scan.prediction_status = "failed"
        scan.status = "failed"
        db.commit()
        logger.error(
            "[PREDICTION_SERVICE] Scan #%d: unexpected prediction error: %s",
            scan_id, exc,
        )
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="An unexpected error occurred during prediction.",
        )


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\recommendation_service.py
================================================================================

﻿import logging
from typing import Optional, List
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database.models.tur_disease_recommendation import TurDiseaseRecommendation
from app.schemas.recommendation import SprayGuidanceResponse

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.services.recommendation")


def _parse_text_to_list(text_val: Optional[str], default_list: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    if not text_val or not text_val.strip():
        return default_list
    lines = [line.strip("- *").strip() for line in text_val.split("\n") if line.strip()]
    return lines if lines else default_list


def get_tur_spray_guidance_service(db: Session, disease_name: str) -> Optional[SprayGuidanceResponse]:
    clean_disease_name = disease_name.strip()
    if not clean_disease_name:
        return None

    query = select(TurDiseaseRecommendation).where(
        TurDiseaseRecommendation.crop == "tur",
        func.lower(TurDiseaseRecommendation.disease_name) == clean_disease_name.lower(),
        TurDiseaseRecommendation.is_active == True,
    )
    rec = db.scalar(query)

    if not rec:
        logger.info("[RECOMMENDATION SERVICE] No active verified recommendation found for crop=tur, disease=%s", clean_disease_name)
        return None

    default_spray_guidance = [
        "01 Prepare the sprayer",
        "02 Follow the recommended treatment",
        "03 Spray the affected Tur plants evenly",
        "04 Avoid unnecessary overspray",
        "05 Keep people and animals away from the spray area",
    ]
    default_after_spraying = [
        "Clean the sprayer properly",
        "Wash hands and exposed areas",
        "Observe the crop regularly",
        "Do not repeat spraying unnecessarily",
    ]
    default_safety_precautions = [
        "Wear gloves and protective clothing",
        "Avoid direct contact with the spray",
        "Keep children and animals away",
        "Follow product-label instructions",
    ]

    spray_guidance_list = _parse_text_to_list(rec.application_method, default_spray_guidance)
    after_spraying_list = _parse_text_to_list(rec.after_spray_advisory, default_after_spraying)
    safety_precautions_list = _parse_text_to_list(rec.safety_precautions, default_safety_precautions)

    leaf_condition = "Needs Treatment"
    if "healthy" in clean_disease_name.lower():
        leaf_condition = "Monitor"

    return SprayGuidanceResponse(
        crop="tur",
        disease_name=rec.disease_name,
        leaf_condition=leaf_condition,
        important_alert="Follow the verified treatment recommendation and product-label instructions while spraying.",
        spray_guidance=spray_guidance_list,
        after_spraying=after_spraying_list,
        safety_precautions=safety_precautions_list,
        farmer_action="Monitor your Tur crop regularly. If symptoms continue, increase, or change, scan a fresh leaf again.",
    )


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\scan_service.py
================================================================================

"""
Scan Service — handles scan record creation, image storage, and voice report linking.

Responsibilities:
- Validate uploaded files before writing to disk.
- Persist images via storage_service (safe filenames, no path traversal).
- Create TurScan records owned by the authenticated user.
- Create TurVoiceReport records linked to the correct scan.
- Enforce user ownership on all scan queries.
"""

import logging
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database.models.tur_scan import TurScan
from app.database.models.tur_voice_report import TurVoiceReport
from app.services.storage_service import (
    save_scan_image,
    save_voice_report,
    validate_audio_upload,
    validate_image_upload,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.scan_service")


# ── Scan creation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_scan_with_image(
    db: Session,
    user_id: int,
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
    original_filename: Optional[str],
    crop: str = "tur",
) -> TurScan:
    """
    Validates, stores the image, and creates a TurScan record in PostgreSQL.

    Raises HTTPException for all validation and storage errors.
    The scan record is created with status="received" and prediction_status="pending".
    """
    # 1. Validate file (raises ValueError on failure)
    try:
        validate_image_upload(file_bytes, content_type)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=str(exc),
        )

    # 2. Persist to filesystem (raises IOError on failure)
    try:
        storage_key, stored_filename = save_scan_image(
            file_bytes=file_bytes,
            content_type=content_type,
            original_filename=original_filename,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("[SCAN_SERVICE] Failed to store scan image: %s", exc)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Image could not be stored. Please try again.",
        )

    # 3. Create database record
    scan = TurScan(
        user_id=user_id,
        crop=crop,
        image_path=storage_key,
        image_filename=original_filename,
        image_content_type=content_type,
        image_size=len(file_bytes),
        status="received",
        prediction_status="pending",
    )
    db.add(scan)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(scan)

    logger.info(
        "[SCAN_SERVICE] TurScan #%d created for user_id=%d crop=%s size=%d",
        scan.id, user_id, crop, len(file_bytes),
    )
    return scan


# ── Scan retrieval ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_scan_for_user(
    db: Session,
    scan_id: int,
    user_id: int,
) -> TurScan:
    """
    Retrieves a TurScan record, enforcing ownership.
    Raises HTTP 404 if scan is not found.
    Raises HTTP 403 if the scan belongs to a different user.
    """
    scan = db.query(TurScan).filter(TurScan.id == scan_id).first()
    if not scan:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Scan #{scan_id} not found.",
        )
    if scan.user_id != user_id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Access denied. This scan belongs to another user.",
        )
    return scan


# ── Voice report creation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_voice_report(
    db: Session,
    scan_id: int,
    user_id: int,
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
    original_filename: Optional[str],
    language: Optional[str] = None,
    duration: Optional[float] = None,
) -> TurVoiceReport:
    """
    Validates, stores the voice recording, and creates a TurVoiceReport record.

    Verifies scan ownership before linking the voice report.
    Raises HTTPException for validation and storage errors.
    """
    # 1. Verify scan exists and belongs to this user
    scan = get_scan_for_user(db=db, scan_id=scan_id, user_id=user_id)

    # 2. Validate audio file
    try:
        validate_audio_upload(file_bytes, content_type)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=str(exc),
        )

    # 3. Persist to filesystem
    try:
        storage_key, stored_filename = save_voice_report(
            file_bytes=file_bytes,
            content_type=content_type,
            original_filename=original_filename,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("[SCAN_SERVICE] Failed to store voice report: %s", exc)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            detail="Voice recording could not be stored. Please try again.",
        )

    # 4. Create database record
    voice_report = TurVoiceReport(
        scan_id=scan.id,
        user_id=user_id,
        audio_path=storage_key,
        audio_filename=original_filename,
        audio_content_type=content_type,
        audio_size=len(file_bytes),
        language=language,
        duration=duration,
        transcription=None,  # Reserved for future speech-to-text integration
    )
    db.add(voice_report)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(voice_report)

    logger.info(
        "[SCAN_SERVICE] TurVoiceReport #%d created for scan_id=%d user_id=%d language=%s",
        voice_report.id, scan_id, user_id, language,
    )
    return voice_report


================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\services\storage_service.py
================================================================================

"""
Storage Service — safe local filesystem storage for uploaded images and audio.

Key security properties:
- Server-side UUID-based filenames prevent path traversal.
- Client-provided filenames are never used directly on disk.
- Only the relative storage key is persisted in the database.
- Absolute paths are never exposed to the mobile client.
"""

import logging
import os
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Tuple

logger = logging.getLogger("tursathi.storage")

# ── Storage root configuration ──────────────────────────────────────────────
# Relative to the backend project root; resolved at import time.
_BACKEND_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
UPLOAD_DIR = _BACKEND_ROOT / "uploads"

# Sub-directories per asset type
SCAN_IMAGES_DIR = UPLOAD_DIR / "scans"
VOICE_REPORTS_DIR = UPLOAD_DIR / "voice_reports"

# ── Allowed MIME types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = {
    "image/jpeg",
    "image/jpg",
    "image/png",
    "image/webp",
    "image/heic",
    "image/heif",
}

ALLOWED_AUDIO_TYPES = {
    "audio/mpeg",
    "audio/mp4",
    "audio/mp4a-latm",
    "audio/x-m4a",
    "audio/wav",
    "audio/x-wav",
    "audio/webm",
    "audio/ogg",
    "audio/aac",
    "audio/3gpp",
    "audio/3gpp2",
}

# Maximum upload sizes
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024   # 20 MB
MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024   # 50 MB

# Extension fallback map (content_type → extension)
_MIME_TO_EXT: dict[str, str] = {
    "image/jpeg": ".jpg",
    "image/jpg": ".jpg",
    "image/png": ".png",
    "image/webp": ".webp",
    "image/heic": ".heic",
    "image/heif": ".heif",
    "audio/mpeg": ".mp3",
    "audio/mp4": ".m4a",
    "audio/mp4a-latm": ".m4a",
    "audio/x-m4a": ".m4a",
    "audio/wav": ".wav",
    "audio/x-wav": ".wav",
    "audio/webm": ".webm",
    "audio/ogg": ".ogg",
    "audio/aac": ".aac",
    "audio/3gpp": ".3gp",
    "audio/3gpp2": ".3g2",
}


def _ensure_dir(path: Path) -> None:
    """Creates directory and all parents if they do not already exist."""
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def _safe_extension(content_type: Optional[str], fallback: str = ".bin") -> str:
    """Returns a file extension for a given MIME type."""
    if not content_type:
        return fallback
    ct = content_type.strip().lower()
    # Strip parameters (e.g. "image/jpeg; charset=utf-8" → "image/jpeg")
    base_ct = ct.split(";")[0].strip()
    return _MIME_TO_EXT.get(base_ct, fallback)


def save_scan_image(
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
    original_filename: Optional[str] = None,
) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Persists an uploaded scan image to the secure uploads directory.

    Parameters
    ----------
    file_bytes      : Raw bytes of the uploaded image.
    content_type    : MIME type reported by the client (validated before calling).
    original_filename : Original filename from the upload (sanitized; not used for disk path).

    Returns
    -------
    (storage_key, filename_on_disk)
        storage_key    — Relative path from UPLOAD_DIR root (stored in DB).
        filename_on_disk — UUID-based filename (not exposed to client).
    """
    _ensure_dir(SCAN_IMAGES_DIR)

    ext = _safe_extension(content_type, fallback=".jpg")
    filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{ext}"
    file_path = SCAN_IMAGES_DIR / filename

    file_path.write_bytes(file_bytes)
    logger.info("[STORAGE] Scan image saved: %s (%d bytes)", filename, len(file_bytes))

    # storage_key is relative so it is portable and safe to store in the DB
    storage_key = f"scans/{filename}"
    return storage_key, filename


def save_voice_report(
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
    original_filename: Optional[str] = None,
) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Persists an uploaded voice recording to the secure uploads directory.

    Returns
    -------
    (storage_key, filename_on_disk)
    """
    _ensure_dir(VOICE_REPORTS_DIR)

    ext = _safe_extension(content_type, fallback=".m4a")
    filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{ext}"
    file_path = VOICE_REPORTS_DIR / filename

    file_path.write_bytes(file_bytes)
    logger.info("[STORAGE] Voice report saved: %s (%d bytes)", filename, len(file_bytes))

    storage_key = f"voice_reports/{filename}"
    return storage_key, filename


def get_absolute_image_path(storage_key: str) -> Path:
    """
    Resolves a storage_key back to an absolute Path on the server filesystem.
    Used internally by the ML prediction service — never exposed to clients.
    """
    path = (UPLOAD_DIR / storage_key).resolve()
    # Guard against path traversal
    if not str(path).startswith(str(UPLOAD_DIR.resolve())):
        raise ValueError("Path traversal detected in storage_key.")
    return path


def validate_image_upload(
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
) -> None:
    """
    Raises ValueError with a human-readable message if the image upload is invalid.
    Call before persisting.
    """
    if not file_bytes:
        raise ValueError("Uploaded image is empty.")

    ct = (content_type or "").strip().lower().split(";")[0].strip()
    if ct not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unsupported image type '{content_type}'. "
            f"Allowed types: JPEG, PNG, WebP, HEIC."
        )

    if len(file_bytes) > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES:
        mb = len(file_bytes) / (1024 * 1024)
        raise ValueError(
            f"Image size {mb:.1f} MB exceeds the maximum allowed 20 MB."
        )


def validate_audio_upload(
    file_bytes: bytes,
    content_type: Optional[str],
) -> None:
    """
    Raises ValueError if the audio upload is invalid.
    """
    if not file_bytes:
        raise ValueError("Uploaded audio is empty.")

    ct = (content_type or "").strip().lower().split(";")[0].strip()
    if ct not in ALLOWED_AUDIO_TYPES:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unsupported audio type '{content_type}'. "
            f"Allowed: MP3, M4A, WAV, WebM, OGG, AAC."
        )

    if len(file_bytes) > MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES:
        mb = len(file_bytes) / (1024 * 1024)
        raise ValueError(
            f"Audio size {mb:.1f} MB exceeds the maximum allowed 50 MB."
        )

import pytest

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

# ── Mock Fixtures ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def mock_scan(scan_id=1, user_id=1, disease_name="Leaf_Spot", confidence=0.75, status="completed"):
    scan = MagicMock(spec=TurScan)
    scan.id = scan_id
    scan.user_id = user_id
    scan.crop = "tur"
    scan.disease_name = disease_name
    scan.confidence = confidence
    scan.severity = "moderate"
    scan.prediction_status = status

def mock_rec(disease_name="Leaf_Spot"):
    rec = MagicMock(spec=TurDiseaseRecommendation)
    rec.crop = "tur"
    rec.disease_name = disease_name
    rec.disease_description = "Leaf spot disease affecting Tur leaves."
    rec.treatment = "Mancozeb 75 WP"
    rec.active_ingredient = "Mancozeb 75% WP"
