    rec.water_quantity = None
    rec.per_pump_quantity = None
    rec.spray_timing = "At onset of disease"
    rec.application_method = "Foliar spray"
    rec.safety_precautions = "Wear protective gear"
    rec.after_spray_advisory = "Monitor crop"
    rec.is_active = True
    return rec

def test_authenticated_ai_guidance_request():
    client = TestClient(app)
    mock_user = MagicMock()
    mock_user.id = 1
    mock_scan_obj = mock_scan(scan_id=1, user_id=1, confidence=0.70)
    mock_scan_obj.prediction_status = "completed"

    from app.api.v1.auth import get_current_user
    from app.database.session import get_db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = lambda: mock_user
    try:
        with patch("app.api.v1.predictions.get_scan_for_user", return_value=mock_scan_obj):
            with patch.object(settings, "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", None):
                res = client.post(
                    "/api/v1/predictions/tur-leaf/1/ai-guidance",
                    headers={"Authorization": "Bearer fake_token"}
                )
                assert res.status_code == 200
                data = res.json()
                assert data["crop"] == "tur"
                assert "status" in data
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


# ── 12, 13, 14. Multilingual Language Tests ───────────────────────────────────

def test_english_language():
    db = MagicMock()
    scan = mock_scan(confidence=0.30)
    res = generate_claude_guidance(db, scan, language="en")
    assert res.language == "en"
    assert "confidence is below 60%" in res.disease_explanation

================================================================================
FILE: datasets\validate_tur_dataset.py
================================================================================

"""
TurSathi AI - Tur Leaf Disease Dataset Validation Script

Validates the Tur leaf disease dataset structure, image counts, image integrity,
dimensions, supported formats, and potential duplicates without modifying or deleting any files.

Classes expected:
- Healthy
- SMD
- Phytophthora_Blight
- Wilt
- Dry_Root_Rot

Target per class: 500 valid images (Total target = 2,500)
"""

import os
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Set
from PIL import Image

# Configuration
EXPECTED_CLASSES = ["Healthy", "SMD", "Phytophthora_Blight", "Wilt", "Dry_Root_Rot"]
TARGET_PER_CLASS = 500
SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".bmp"}
DATASET_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "tur"

def compute_file_hash(filepath: Path, block_size: int = 65536) -> str:
    """Computes SHA256 hash of a file for duplicate detection."""
    hasher = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(filepath, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(block_size), b""):
            hasher.update(chunk)
    return hasher.hexdigest()

def validate_dataset(dataset_dir: Path):
    print("=" * 65)
    print("      TUR SATHI AI — DATASET VALIDATION REPORT")
    print("=" * 65)
    print(f"Dataset Directory: {dataset_dir.resolve()}\n")

    if not dataset_dir.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: Dataset directory '{dataset_dir}' does not exist!")
        return

    class_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
    valid_class_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
    corrupted_files: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []
    unsupported_files: List[Path] = []
    hashes: Dict[str, List[Path]] = {}
    duplicates: Dict[str, List[Path]] = {}
    image_dimensions: List[Tuple[int, int]] = []
    missing_classes: List[str] = []
    empty_classes: List[str] = []

    # Check for expected class folders
    for class_name in EXPECTED_CLASSES:
        class_folder = dataset_dir / class_name
        if not class_folder.exists() or not class_folder.is_dir():
            missing_classes.append(class_name)
            continue
        
        files = [f for f in class_folder.iterdir() if f.is_file()]
        class_counts[class_name] = len(files)
        valid_count = 0

        if len(files) == 0:
            empty_classes.append(class_name)
            valid_class_counts[class_name] = 0
            continue

        for file_path in files:
            ext = file_path.suffix.lower()
            if ext not in SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS:
                unsupported_files.append(file_path)
                continue

            # Check duplicate hash
            try:
                file_hash = compute_file_hash(file_path)
                if file_hash in hashes:
                    if file_hash not in duplicates:
                        duplicates[file_hash] = [hashes[file_hash][0]]
                    duplicates[file_hash].append(file_path)
                else:
                    hashes[file_hash] = [file_path]
            except Exception as e:
                corrupted_files.append((file_path, f"Read error: {str(e)}"))
                continue

            # Verify image integrity and dimensions
            try:
                with Image.open(file_path) as img:
                    img.verify()  # Verify image header/integrity
                # Re-open after verify() as recommended by PIL docs
                with Image.open(file_path) as img:
                    width, height = img.size
                    image_dimensions.append((width, height))
                    valid_count += 1
            except Exception as e:
                corrupted_files.append((file_path, f"Corrupt image: {str(e)}"))

        valid_class_counts[class_name] = valid_count

    # --- REPORT GENERATION ---
    print("--- 1. CLASS DIRECTORY & IMAGE COUNTS ---")
    total_raw_files = sum(class_counts.values())
    total_valid_images = sum(valid_class_counts.values())

    for c in EXPECTED_CLASSES:
        raw_c = class_counts.get(c, 0)
        valid_c = valid_class_counts.get(c, 0)
        status_str = f"VALID: {valid_c}/{TARGET_PER_CLASS}"
        if valid_c < TARGET_PER_CLASS:
            shortage = TARGET_PER_CLASS - valid_c
            status_str += f"  (SHORTAGE: {shortage} images needed)"
        elif valid_c == TARGET_PER_CLASS:
            status_str += "  [TARGET REACHED EXACTLY]"
        else:
            status_str += f"  (EXCEEDS TARGET BY {valid_c - TARGET_PER_CLASS})"

        print(f"  - {c:<22}: {status_str}")

    print(f"\n  Total Raw Files Across All Classes: {total_raw_files}")
    print(f"  Total Valid Images Verified       : {total_valid_images} / 2500")

    print("\n--- 2. DATA QUALITY & INTEGRITY ISSUES ---")
    print(f"  - Missing Class Directories : {missing_classes if missing_classes else 'None'}")
    print(f"  - Empty Class Directories   : {empty_classes if empty_classes else 'None'}")
    print(f"  - Corrupted / Unreadable    : {len(corrupted_files)}")
    for file_path, reason in corrupted_files:
        print(f"      * {file_path.relative_to(dataset_dir)} -> {reason}")

    print(f"  - Unsupported Formats       : {len(unsupported_files)}")
    for file_path in unsupported_files:
        print(f"      * {file_path.relative_to(dataset_dir)}")

    print(f"  - Duplicate Image Groups    : {len(duplicates)}")
    for file_hash, dup_list in list(duplicates.items())[:10]: # limit print to 10 groups
        dup_names = [str(p.relative_to(dataset_dir)) for p in dup_list]
        print(f"      * Hash {file_hash[:8]}... : {dup_names}")
    if len(duplicates) > 10:
        print(f"      ... and {len(duplicates) - 10} more duplicate groups.")

    print("\n--- 3. IMAGE DIMENSIONS STATS ---")
    if image_dimensions:
        widths = [w for w, h in image_dimensions]
        heights = [h for w, h in image_dimensions]
        min_w, max_w, avg_w = min(widths), max(widths), sum(widths) // len(widths)
        min_h, max_h, avg_h = min(heights), max(heights), sum(heights) // len(heights)
        print(f"  - Width  (Min / Max / Avg) : {min_w}px / {max_w}px / {avg_w}px")
        print(f"  - Height (Min / Max / Avg) : {min_h}px / {max_h}px / {avg_h}px")
    else:
        print("  - No valid images available to calculate dimensions.")

    print("\n--- 4. TARGET READINESS SUMMARY ---")
    all_classes_ready = all(valid_class_counts.get(c, 0) >= TARGET_PER_CLASS for c in EXPECTED_CLASSES)
    if all_classes_ready:
        print("  [OK] DATASET STATUS: READY FOR MODEL TRAINING APPROVAL")
        print("       All 5 classes have met or exceeded the 500 valid image requirement.")
    else:
        print("  [!] DATASET STATUS: INCOMPLETE (Awaiting genuine images)")
        print("      Model training CANNOT be started yet. Please populate the class folders with genuine, labeled images.")

    print("=" * 65)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    validate_dataset(DATASET_DIR)


================================================================================
FILE: ml\inference\predict_pigeonpea.py
================================================================================

import torch
from torchvision import models, transforms
from PIL import Image
from pathlib import Path

MODEL_PATH = Path("models/best_pigeonpea_disease_final_efficientnet_b0.pth")

def load_model():
    checkpoint = torch.load(
        MODEL_PATH,
        map_location="cpu",
        weights_only=False
    )

    classes = checkpoint["classes"]

    model = models.efficientnet_b0(weights=None)
    model.classifier[1] = torch.nn.Linear(
        model.classifier[1].in_features,
        len(classes)
    )

    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model_state_dict"])
    model.eval()

    return model, classes


transform = transforms.Compose([
    transforms.Resize((224, 224)),
    transforms.ToTensor(),
    transforms.Normalize(
        [0.485, 0.456, 0.406],
        [0.229, 0.224, 0.225]
    )
])


def predict(image_path):
    model, classes = load_model()

    image = Image.open(image_path).convert("RGB")
    image_tensor = transform(image).unsqueeze(0)

    with torch.no_grad():
        output = model(image_tensor)
        probabilities = torch.softmax(output, dim=1)

    confidence, predicted_index = torch.max(probabilities, 1)

    predicted_class = classes[predicted_index.item()]
    confidence_value = confidence.item() * 100

    print("\n==============================")
    print("   TurSathi AI Prediction")
    print("==============================")
    print(f"Image      : {image_path}")
    print(f"Prediction : {predicted_class}")
    print(f"Confidence : {confidence_value:.2f}%")
    print("==============================\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    image_path = input("Enter Tur leaf image path: ").strip().strip('"')

    if not Path(image_path).exists():
        print("ERROR: Image file not found.")
    else:
        predict(image_path)


================================================================================
FILE: ml\training\make_duplicate_review.py
================================================================================

﻿from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image, ImageOps, ImageDraw
import hashlib
import csv
import math

SOURCE = Path("PPLD_extracted/PPLD")
OUT = Path("reports/duplicate_review")
OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

rows = []

for class_dir in SOURCE.iterdir():
    if not class_dir.is_dir():
        continue

    for file in class_dir.iterdir():
        if file.is_file() and file.suffix.lower() in [".jpg",".jpeg",".png",".webp"]:
            try:
                h = hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                rows.append({
                    "hash": h,
                    "class": class_dir.name,
                    "path": str(file.resolve())
                })
            except Exception as e:
                print("READ ERROR:", file, e)

groups = {}

for row in rows:
    groups.setdefault(row["hash"], []).append(row)

conflicts = []

for h, group in groups.items():
    classes = sorted(set(x["class"] for x in group))
    if len(classes) > 1:
        conflicts.append((h, group))

conflicts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

csv_path = OUT / "cross_class_duplicates.csv"

with csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    writer = csv.writer(f)
    writer.writerow(["Group","SHA256","Classes","File"])

    for i, (h, group) in enumerate(conflicts, 1):
        classes = " | ".join(sorted(set(x["class"] for x in group)))
        for item in group:
            writer.writerow([i, h, classes, item["path"]])

print("Cross-class duplicate groups:", len(conflicts))
print("CSV:", csv_path)

# Create visual sheets
PER_SHEET = 15
COLS = 3
TILE_W = 360
TILE_H = 330

for sheet_start in range(0, len(conflicts), PER_SHEET):

    batch = conflicts[sheet_start:sheet_start + PER_SHEET]
    sheet_no = sheet_start // PER_SHEET + 1
    rows_count = math.ceil(len(batch) / COLS)

    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (COLS * TILE_W, rows_count * TILE_H), "white")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)

    for local_index, (h, group) in enumerate(batch):

        x = (local_index % COLS) * TILE_W
        y = (local_index // COLS) * TILE_H

        # Same image content appears in multiple classes.
        # Load only the first copy for visual inspection.
        image_path = Path(group[0]["path"])

        try:
            img = Image.open(image_path).convert("RGB")
            img.thumbnail((330, 245))

            px = x + (TILE_W - img.width) // 2
            py = y + 10

            sheet.paste(img, (px, py))

            group_no = sheet_start + local_index + 1
            classes = " / ".join(sorted(set(item["class"] for item in group)))

            draw.text(
                (x + 10, y + 265),
                f"GROUP {group_no}",
                fill="black"
            )

            draw.text(
                (x + 10, y + 285),
                classes,
                fill="black"
            )

            draw.text(
                (x + 10, y + 305),
                f"Copies: {len(group)}",
                fill="black"
            )

        except Exception as e:
            draw.text(
                (x + 10, y + 20),
                f"IMAGE ERROR: {image_path.name}",
                fill="red"
            )
            print("IMAGE ERROR:", image_path, e)

    output = OUT / f"duplicate_sheet_{sheet_no:02d}.jpg"
    sheet.save(output, quality=95)
    print("Created:", output)

print("\nVISUAL REVIEW COMPLETE")
print("NO FILES WERE DELETED.")


================================================================================
FILE: ml\training\remove_exact_duplicates.py
================================================================================

﻿from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import shutil

SOURCE = Path("PPLD_extracted/PPLD")
BACKUP = Path("PPLD_extracted/PPLD_backup_before_duplicate_removal")

rows = []

for class_dir in SOURCE.iterdir():
    if not class_dir.is_dir():
        continue

    for file in class_dir.iterdir():
        if file.is_file() and file.suffix.lower() in [".jpg",".jpeg",".png",".webp"]:
            try:
                h = hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

                rows.append({
                    "hash": h,
                    "class": class_dir.name,
                    "path": file
                })

            except Exception as e:
                print("READ ERROR:", file, e)

groups = {}

for row in rows:
    groups.setdefault(row["hash"], []).append(row)

deleted = []
kept = []

for h, group in groups.items():

    if len(group) <= 1:
        continue

    classes = set(item["class"] for item in group)

    # Cross-class exact duplicate:
    # remove every copy because the correct label cannot be inferred safely.
    if len(classes) > 1:

        for item in group:
            if item["path"].exists():
                deleted.append(str(item["path"]))
                item["path"].unlink()

    # Same-class exact duplicate:
    # keep first, remove remaining copies.
    else:

        group = sorted(group, key=lambda x: str(x["path"]))

        kept.append(str(group[0]["path"]))

        for item in group[1:]:
            if item["path"].exists():
                deleted.append(str(item["path"]))
                item["path"].unlink()

print()
print("=" * 70)
print("DUPLICATE REMOVAL COMPLETE")
print("=" * 70)

print(f"Files deleted : {len(deleted)}")
print(f"Files kept as duplicate representative : {len(kept)}")

print()
print("Backup remains at:")
print(BACKUP)

print()
print("Deleted files:")
for path in deleted:
    print(path)

print()
print("Original source cleaned.")
print("Backup was NOT modified.")
print("Clean training dataset was NOT modified.")


================================================================================
FILE: ml\training\train_pigeonpea_final.py
================================================================================

import os
import sys
import json
import random
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torchvision import datasets, transforms, models
from sklearn.metrics import classification_report, confusion_matrix
import time
from pathlib import Path

# Fix random seed for reproducibility
SEED = 42
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
DATA_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "datasets" / "pigeonpea_disease_final"
MODELS_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / "models"
MODELS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

MODEL_SAVE_PATH = MODELS_DIR / "best_pigeonpea_disease_final_efficientnet_b0.pth"
RESULTS_SAVE_PATH = MODELS_DIR / "pigeonpea_disease_final_training_results.json"
CM_SAVE_PATH = MODELS_DIR / "pigeonpea_disease_final_confusion_matrix.txt"

# Parameters
BATCH_SIZE = 16
NUM_WORKERS = 2
EPOCHS = 20
IMG_SIZE = 224
EXPECTED_CLASSES = ["Healthy", "Leaf_Spot", "Leaf_webber", "Sterilic_mosaic", "Wilt"]

def check_cuda():
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        print("ERROR: CUDA is not available. GPU is required for this training script. Stopping.")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    device = torch.device("cuda")
    gpu_name = torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)
    vram = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory / (1024**3)
    
    print(f"DEVICE: CUDA")
    print(f"GPU: {gpu_name}")
    print(f"VRAM: {vram:.2f} GB")
    return device

def get_dataloaders():
    train_dir = DATA_DIR / "train"
    val_dir = DATA_DIR / "val"
    test_dir = DATA_DIR / "test"
    
    if not train_dir.exists() or not val_dir.exists() or not test_dir.exists():
        print("ERROR: Missing train, val, or test directories in dataset.")
        sys.exit(1)

    train_transforms = transforms.Compose([
        transforms.Resize((IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE)),
        transforms.RandomHorizontalFlip(),
        transforms.RandomRotation(15),
        transforms.ColorJitter(brightness=0.2, contrast=0.2, saturation=0.2),
        transforms.ToTensor(),
        transforms.Normalize([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], [0.229, 0.224, 0.225])
    ])
    
    eval_transforms = transforms.Compose([
        transforms.Resize((IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE)),
        transforms.ToTensor(),
        transforms.Normalize([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], [0.229, 0.224, 0.225])
    ])

    train_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(train_dir, transform=train_transforms)
    val_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(val_dir, transform=eval_transforms)
    test_dataset = datasets.ImageFolder(test_dir, transform=eval_transforms)

    # Class verification
    if len(train_dataset.classes) != 5:
        print(f"ERROR: Expected exactly 5 classes, found {len(train_dataset.classes)}")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    for cls in EXPECTED_CLASSES:
        if cls not in train_dataset.classes:
            print(f"ERROR: Expected class {cls} not found in train dataset.")
            sys.exit(1)
            
    if train_dataset.classes != val_dataset.classes or train_dataset.classes != test_dataset.classes:
        print("ERROR: Class order differs between train, val, and test datasets.")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    print(f"CLASSES: {len(train_dataset.classes)}")
    print(f"Class names and indices: {train_dataset.class_to_idx}")
    print(f"TRAIN: {len(train_dataset)}")
    print(f"VAL: {len(val_dataset)}")
    print(f"TEST: {len(test_dataset)}")

    train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=NUM_WORKERS, pin_memory=True)
    val_loader = DataLoader(val_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=NUM_WORKERS, pin_memory=True)
    test_loader = DataLoader(test_dataset, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=NUM_WORKERS, pin_memory=True)

    return train_loader, val_loader, test_loader, train_dataset.classes

def main():
    device = check_cuda()
    
    train_loader, val_loader, test_loader, classes = get_dataloaders()
    num_classes = len(classes)

    model = models.efficientnet_b0(weights=models.EfficientNet_B0_Weights.DEFAULT)
    num_ftrs = model.classifier[1].in_features
    model.classifier[1] = nn.Linear(num_ftrs, num_classes)
    model = model.to(device)

    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()
    optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3)
    scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(optimizer, mode='max', factor=0.1, patience=3)

    best_val_acc = 0.0
    best_epoch = 0
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda')

    results = {
        "train_loss": [],
        "train_acc": [],
        "val_loss": [],
        "val_acc": []
    }

    print("\nStarting Training...")
    for epoch in range(EPOCHS):
        # Training Phase
        model.train()
        running_loss = 0.0
        corrects = 0
        total = 0
        
        for inputs, labels in train_loader:
            inputs, labels = inputs.to(device), labels.to(device)
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            
            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda'):
                outputs = model(inputs)
                loss = criterion(outputs, labels)
                
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            scaler.step(optimizer)
            scaler.update()
            
            running_loss += loss.item() * inputs.size(0)
            _, preds = torch.max(outputs, 1)
            corrects += torch.sum(preds == labels.data)
            total += inputs.size(0)
            
        epoch_loss = running_loss / total
        epoch_acc = corrects.double() / total
        
        # Validation Phase
        model.eval()
        val_running_loss = 0.0
        val_corrects = 0
        val_total = 0
        
        with torch.no_grad():
            for inputs, labels in val_loader:
                inputs, labels = inputs.to(device), labels.to(device)
                
                with torch.amp.autocast('cuda'):
                    outputs = model(inputs)
                    loss = criterion(outputs, labels)
                    
                val_running_loss += loss.item() * inputs.size(0)
                _, preds = torch.max(outputs, 1)
                val_corrects += torch.sum(preds == labels.data)
                val_total += inputs.size(0)
                
        val_loss = val_running_loss / val_total
        val_acc = val_corrects.double() / val_total
        
        scheduler.step(val_acc)
        
        print(f"Epoch {epoch+1}/{EPOCHS} | Train Loss: {epoch_loss:.4f} Acc: {epoch_acc:.4f} | Val Loss: {val_loss:.4f} Acc: {val_acc:.4f}")
        
        results["train_loss"].append(epoch_loss)
        results["train_acc"].append(epoch_acc.item())
        results["val_loss"].append(val_loss)
        results["val_acc"].append(val_acc.item())
        
        # Save Best Model
        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            best_epoch = epoch + 1
            checkpoint = {
                "model_state_dict": model.state_dict(),
                "classes": classes,
                "num_classes": num_classes,
                "best_val_accuracy": best_val_acc.item(),
                "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                "image_size": IMG_SIZE,
                "model_name": "efficientnet_b0"
            }
            torch.save(checkpoint, MODEL_SAVE_PATH)
            print(f"  -> Best model saved at epoch {best_epoch} with Val Acc: {best_val_acc:.4f}")
            
    print("\nTraining Complete.")
    
    # ---------------------------
    # Final Evaluation on Test Set
    # ---------------------------
    print("\nEvaluating on Test Set with Best Model...")
    checkpoint = torch.load(MODEL_SAVE_PATH)
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model_state_dict"])
    model.eval()
    
    test_running_loss = 0.0
    test_corrects = 0
    test_total = 0
    all_preds = []
    all_labels = []
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        for inputs, labels in test_loader:
            inputs, labels = inputs.to(device), labels.to(device)
            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda'):
                outputs = model(inputs)
                loss = criterion(outputs, labels)
            
            test_running_loss += loss.item() * inputs.size(0)
            _, preds = torch.max(outputs, 1)
            test_corrects += torch.sum(preds == labels.data)
            test_total += inputs.size(0)
            
            all_preds.extend(preds.cpu().numpy())
            all_labels.extend(labels.cpu().numpy())
            
    test_loss = test_running_loss / test_total
    test_acc = test_corrects.double() / test_total
    
    print(f"\nTest Loss: {test_loss:.4f}")
    print(f"Test Accuracy: {test_acc:.4f}")
    
    report = classification_report(all_labels, all_preds, target_names=classes, digits=4)
    print("\nClassification Report:\n", report)
    
    cm = confusion_matrix(all_labels, all_preds)
    print("\nConfusion Matrix:\n", cm)
    
    with open(CM_SAVE_PATH, "w") as f:
        f.write("Confusion Matrix:\n")
        f.write(np.array2string(cm, separator=', '))
        f.write("\n\nClassification Report:\n")
        f.write(report)
        
    results["test_loss"] = test_loss
    results["test_acc"] = test_acc.item()
    results["best_val_accuracy"] = best_val_acc.item()
    results["best_epoch"] = best_epoch
    
    with open(RESULTS_SAVE_PATH, "w") as f:
        json.dump(results, f, indent=4)
        
    print(f"\nResults saved to {RESULTS_SAVE_PATH}")
    print(f"Confusion matrix saved to {CM_SAVE_PATH}")
    
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: project_export.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# ============================================================================
# PROJECT EXTRACTION UTILITY
# ============================================================================
# Purpose: Generate a comprehensive report of the entire Python codebase
# Output: Single text file containing:
#         1. Visual tree structure of project directories/files
#         2. Complete content of all Python files
#         3. Summary statistics
# Usage: python Project_extract.py
# Use Case: Code review, documentation, sharing codebase overview
# ============================================================================

"""
Extract all Python files with their content and project structure into a single text file.
"""

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def get_project_structure(root_dir, prefix="", max_depth=10, current_depth=0, ignore_dirs=None):
    """
    Generate a text representation of the project structure.
    
    Creates a tree-style visualization of directories and files.
    Recursively traverses subdirectories while respecting depth limits.
    
    Args:
        root_dir: Starting directory path
        prefix: String prefix for tree indentation
        max_depth: Maximum recursion depth to prevent infinite loops
        current_depth: Current recursion level
        ignore_dirs: Set of directory names to skip
    
    Returns:
        String containing formatted directory tree
    """
    if ignore_dirs is None:
        ignore_dirs = {".git", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", "*.egg-info", ".venv", "venv", "env", ".csv", "node_modules", "dist", "build", ".expo"}
    
    if current_depth >= max_depth:
        return ""
    
    structure = ""
    try:
        items = sorted(os.listdir(root_dir))
    except PermissionError:
        return ""
    
    dirs = []
    files = []
    
    for item in items:
        # Skip hidden files/dirs and common ignore patterns
        if item.startswith("."):
            continue
        if item in ignore_dirs or any(item.endswith(x.replace("*", "")) for x in ignore_dirs if "*" in x):
            continue
        
        item_path = os.path.join(root_dir, item)
        if os.path.isdir(item_path):
            dirs.append(item)
        else:
            files.append(item)
    
    # Add files first
    for i, file in enumerate(files):
        is_last_file = (i == len(files) - 1) and len(dirs) == 0
        structure += f"{prefix}{'└── ' if is_last_file else '├── '}{file}\n"
    
    # Add directories
    for i, dir_name in enumerate(dirs):
        is_last = i == len(dirs) - 1
        structure += f"{prefix}{'└── ' if is_last else '├── '}{dir_name}/\n"
        
        dir_path = os.path.join(root_dir, dir_name)
        extension = "    " if is_last else "│   "
        structure += get_project_structure(dir_path, prefix + extension, max_depth, current_depth + 1, ignore_dirs)
    
    return structure


def extract_python_files(root_dir, output_file):
    """
    Extract all Python files and their content.
    
    Main extraction function that:
    1. Walks through directory tree to find all .py files
    2. Generates project structure visualization
    3. Writes formatted output with all file contents
    4. Provides summary statistics
    
    Args:
        root_dir: Root directory to scan
        output_file: Path to output text file
    """
    py_files = []
    
    # Collect all Python files
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
        # Skip certain directories
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in {".git", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".venv", "venv", "env", ".csv", "node_modules", "dist", "build", ".expo"}]
        
        for file in files:
            if file.endswith(".py"):
                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                py_files.append(file_path)
    
    # Sort files for consistent output
    py_files.sort()
    
    # Write to output file
    with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        # Header
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write("PROJECT EXTRACTION REPORT\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")
        
        # Project Structure
        out.write("PROJECT STRUCTURE:\n")
        out.write("-" * 80 + "\n\n")
        out.write(f"{os.path.basename(root_dir)}/\n")
        out.write(get_project_structure(root_dir))
        out.write("\n\n")
        
        # File Contents
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write("FILE CONTENTS\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")
        
        for file_path in py_files:
            # Calculate relative path
            rel_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, root_dir)
            
            # Write file header
            out.write(f"\n{'=' * 80}\n")
            out.write(f"FILE: {rel_path}\n")
            out.write(f"{'=' * 80}\n\n")
            
            try:
                with open(file_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    content = f.read()
                    out.write(content)
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"[ERROR] Could not read file: {str(e)}\n")
            
            out.write("\n")
        
        # Summary
        out.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write(f"SUMMARY: {len(py_files)} Python files extracted\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
    
    print(f"✓ Extraction complete!")
    print(f"✓ Total Python files: {len(py_files)}")
    print(f"✓ Output saved to: {output_file}")
    print(f"✓ File size: {os.path.getsize(output_file) / 1024:.2f} KB")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Get project root (current directory)
    project_root = os.getcwd()
    
    # Output file
    output_filename = "project_extraction.txt"
    output_path = os.path.join(project_root, output_filename)
    
    print(f"Extracting Python files from: {project_root}")
    print(f"Output file: {output_path}\n")
    
    extract_python_files(project_root, output_path)

﻿# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
TurSathi AI - Balanced Pigeonpea Disease Dataset Preparation
Target: 500 images/class  (train=300, val=100, test=100)
Author: TurSathi AI Dataset Pipeline  |  Date: 2026-10-04
"""
import sys, os, shutil, hashlib, random, traceback
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image

if sys.platform == "win32":
    import io
    sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    sys.stderr = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stderr.buffer, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

RANDOM_SEED      = 42
PROJECT_ROOT     = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUTPUT_DIR       = PROJECT_ROOT / "datasets" / "pigeonpea_disease_final"
STAGING_DIR      = PROJECT_ROOT / "datasets" / "_staging"  # temp pool per class
CLASSES          = ["Healthy", "Leaf_Spot", "Leaf_webber", "Sterilic_mosaic", "Wilt"]
TARGET_PER_CLASS = 500
TARGET_TRAIN     = 300
TARGET_VAL       = 100
TARGET_TEST      = 100
SPLITS           = ["train", "val", "test"]
VALID_EXT        = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".bmp", ".tiff", ".tif", ".webp"}

# External source directories (never cleared by this script)
EXTERNAL_SOURCES = {
    "Healthy": [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "train" / "Healthy",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "val"   / "Healthy",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "test"  / "Healthy",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD_backup_before_duplicate_removal" / "Healthy",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD"  / "Healthy",
    ],
    "Leaf_Spot": [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "train" / "Leaf_Spot",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "val"   / "Leaf_Spot",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "test"  / "Leaf_Spot",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD_backup_before_duplicate_removal" / "Leaf_Spot",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD"  / "Leaf_Spot",
    ],
    "Leaf_webber": [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "train" / "Leaf_webber",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "val"   / "Leaf_webber",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "test"  / "Leaf_webber",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD_backup_before_duplicate_removal" / "Leaf_webber",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD"  / "Leaf_webber",
    ],
    "Sterilic_mosaic": [
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "train" / "Sterilic_mosaic",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "val"   / "Sterilic_mosaic",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_processed" / "test"  / "Sterilic_mosaic",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD_backup_before_duplicate_removal" / "Sterilic_mosaic",
        PROJECT_ROOT / "PPLD_extracted" / "PPLD"  / "Sterilic_mosaic",
    ],
    "Wilt": [],  # No external Wilt source - only what's in OUTPUT_DIR
}


def sha256_file(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(str(path), "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def is_valid_image(path):
    try:
        with Image.open(str(path)) as img:
            img.verify()
        with Image.open(str(path)) as img:
            img.load()
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def collect_from_dirs(dirs):
    imgs = []
    for d in dirs:
        d = Path(d)
        if not d.exists():
            continue
        for f in sorted(d.iterdir()):
            if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in VALID_EXT:
                imgs.append(f)
    return imgs


def dedup(images):
    seen, unique, dupes = set(), [], 0
    for img in images:
        h = sha256_file(img)
        if h not in seen:
            seen.add(h); unique.append(img)
        else:
            dupes += 1
    print(f"    Dedup: {len(images)} -> {len(unique)} unique ({dupes} removed)")
    return unique


def verify(images):
    valid, bad = [], []
    for img in images:
        (valid if is_valid_image(img) else bad).append(img)
    if bad:
        print(f"    WARNING: {len(bad)} corrupted images skipped")
    return valid, bad


def copy_to(imgs, out_dir, prefix, split_name):
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for i, src in enumerate(imgs):
        ext = Path(src).suffix.lower()
        shutil.copy2(str(src), str(out_dir / f"{prefix}_{split_name}_{i:04d}{ext}"))


def main():
    random.seed(RANDOM_SEED)
    print("=" * 70)
    print("TurSathi AI - Balanced Dataset Preparation")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Root   : {PROJECT_ROOT}")
    print(f"Output : {OUTPUT_DIR}")
    print(f"Target : {TARGET_PER_CLASS}/class (train={TARGET_TRAIN}, val={TARGET_VAL}, test={TARGET_TEST})")
    print(f"Seed   : {RANDOM_SEED}")
    print()

    # ── STEP 0: Stage ALL current output images to safe temp dirs ───────────
    # This prevents the "source deleted before copy" bug.
    print("STEP 0: Staging current output images to safe temp folders...")
    if STAGING_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(STAGING_DIR)
    STAGING_DIR.mkdir(parents=True)

    staged_per_class = {}
    for cls in CLASSES:
        cls_stage = STAGING_DIR / cls
        cls_stage.mkdir()
        count = 0
        for split in SPLITS:
            src_dir = OUTPUT_DIR / split / cls
            if not src_dir.exists():
                continue
            for f in sorted(src_dir.iterdir()):
                if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in VALID_EXT:
                    # Unique name: split_originalname
                    dst = cls_stage / f"{split}_{f.name}"
                    shutil.copy2(str(f), str(dst))
                    count += 1
        staged_per_class[cls] = count
        print(f"  {cls}: staged {count} images")
    print()

    shortage_report = {}
    class_results   = {}

    for cls in CLASSES:
        print("-" * 70)
        print(f"  CLASS: {cls}")
        print("-" * 70)

        # Sources = staged output + external sources
        all_source_dirs = [STAGING_DIR / cls] + EXTERNAL_SOURCES.get(cls, [])
        candidates = collect_from_dirs(all_source_dirs)
        print(f"  [1] Raw candidates : {len(candidates)}")

        print("  [2] Verifying...")
        valid, bad = verify(candidates)
        print(f"      Valid={len(valid)}  Corrupted={len(bad)}")

        print("  [3] Deduplicating...")
        unique = dedup(valid)
        print(f"      Unique: {len(unique)}")

        if len(unique) < TARGET_PER_CLASS:
            shortage = TARGET_PER_CLASS - len(unique)
            shortage_report[cls] = {
                "available": len(unique),
                "target": TARGET_PER_CLASS,
                "shortage": shortage,
            }
            print(f"\n  *** SHORTAGE: {cls} = {len(unique)}/{TARGET_PER_CLASS} (need {shortage} more) ***")
            usable = len(unique)
            n_train = int(usable * 0.60)
            n_val   = int(usable * 0.20)
            n_test  = usable - n_train - n_val
        else:
            usable = TARGET_PER_CLASS
            n_train, n_val, n_test = TARGET_TRAIN, TARGET_VAL, TARGET_TEST
            print(f"  OK: Using {usable} of {len(unique)} available.")

        random.seed(RANDOM_SEED)
        random.shuffle(unique)
        selected = unique[:usable]
        train_imgs = selected[:n_train]
        val_imgs   = selected[n_train: n_train + n_val]
        test_imgs  = selected[n_train + n_val:]
        print(f"  [4] Split: train={len(train_imgs)}, val={len(val_imgs)}, test={len(test_imgs)}")

        # Clear & rebuild output
        prefix = cls.lower()
        out = {sp: OUTPUT_DIR / sp / cls for sp in SPLITS}
        for d in out.values():
            if d.exists(): shutil.rmtree(d)
            d.mkdir(parents=True)

        copy_to(train_imgs, out["train"], prefix, "train")
        copy_to(val_imgs,   out["val"],   prefix, "val")
        copy_to(test_imgs,  out["test"],  prefix, "test")

        a = {sp: len(list(out[sp].iterdir())) for sp in SPLITS}
        a["total"] = sum(a.values())
        class_results[cls] = a
        print(f"  DONE: train={a['train']}, val={a['val']}, test={a['test']}, total={a['total']}")
        print()

    # Cleanup staging
    if STAGING_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(STAGING_DIR)
    print("Staging temp cleaned up.")
    print()

    # ── Cross-split leakage check ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    print("=" * 70)
    print("CROSS-SPLIT LEAKAGE CHECK (SHA256)")
    print("=" * 70)
    leakage = False
    for cls in CLASSES:
        hashes = {sp: set() for sp in SPLITS}
        for sp in SPLITS:
            d = OUTPUT_DIR / sp / cls
            if d.exists():
                for f in d.iterdir():
                    if f.is_file():
                        hashes[sp].add(sha256_file(f))
        tv = hashes["train"] & hashes["val"]
        tt = hashes["train"] & hashes["test"]
        vt = hashes["val"]   & hashes["test"]
        if tv or tt or vt:
            leakage = True
            print(f"  FAIL {cls}: tv={len(tv)}, tt={len(tt)}, vt={len(vt)}")
        else:
            print(f"  OK   {cls}: No leakage")

    # ── Summary ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("FINAL DATASET SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"{'Class':<20} {'Train':>6} {'Val':>6} {'Test':>6} {'Total':>7}  Status")
    print("-" * 60)
    gt = gv = gte = gtotal = 0
    for cls in CLASSES:
        r  = class_results.get(cls, {sp:0 for sp in SPLITS+["total"]})
        ok = (r["train"]==TARGET_TRAIN and r["val"]==TARGET_VAL and r["test"]==TARGET_TEST)
        print(f"{cls:<20} {r['train']:>6} {r['val']:>6} {r['test']:>6} {r['total']:>7}  {'OK' if ok else 'SHORTAGE'}")
        gt += r["train"]; gv += r["val"]; gte += r["test"]; gtotal += r["total"]
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"{'TOTAL':<20} {gt:>6} {gv:>6} {gte:>6} {gtotal:>7}")
    print(f"\nExpected: {TARGET_TRAIN*5} / {TARGET_VAL*5} / {TARGET_TEST*5} / {TARGET_PER_CLASS*5}")

    # ── Shortage report ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if shortage_report:
        print()
        print("=" * 70)
        print("SHORTAGE REPORT")
        print("=" * 70)
        for cls, info in shortage_report.items():
            print(f"  {cls}: available={info['available']}, shortage={info['shortage']}")
            print(f"    --> Collect {info['shortage']} more verified '{cls}' images and rerun.")
        print()
        print("  *** DO NOT TRAIN -- dataset is IMBALANCED. ***")
    else:
        print("\n  ALL CLASSES BALANCED (500 each). Dataset READY.")

    # ── Validation checks ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("VALIDATION CHECKS")
    print("=" * 70)
    passed = total_c = 0

    def chk(label, cond):
        nonlocal passed, total_c
        total_c += 1
        mark = "PASS" if cond else "FAIL"
        print(f"  [{mark}] {label}")
        if cond: passed += 1

    chk("Grand total correct or shortage reported",
        gtotal == TARGET_PER_CLASS * 5 or bool(shortage_report))
    chk("No cross-split leakage", not leakage)
    chk("All 5 classes processed", len(class_results) == 5)
    for sp in SPLITS:
        for cls in CLASSES:
            d = OUTPUT_DIR / sp / cls
            chk(f"Folder exists: {sp}/{cls}", d.exists())
            if d.exists() and cls not in shortage_report:
                count = len([f for f in d.iterdir() if f.is_file()])
                tgt = TARGET_TRAIN if sp=="train" else TARGET_VAL if sp=="val" else TARGET_TEST
                chk(f"Count correct: {sp}/{cls} ({count}/{tgt})", count == tgt)

    print(f"\n  Checks: {passed}/{total_c} passed")
    print()
    if shortage_report:
        print("INCOMPLETE -- Resolve shortages before training.")
        sys.exit(1)
    else:
        print("COMPLETE -- All checks passed!")
        sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nAborted.")
        sys.exit(2)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\nFATAL: {e}")
        traceback.print_exc()
        sys.exit(3)
